//! Deployment purpose belongs to the store, never to application source or a seed plan.
use std::path::Path;
use crate::store_owner::Owner;

pub(super) const KEY: &str = "deployment_environment";
fn production() -> String {
    "E_STAGING: existing production/legacy stores cannot be relabelled; deploy --staging into a separate fresh directory".into()
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#1-commission-and-boundary — classify only a fresh store under its existing exclusive owner.
pub(super) fn classify(dir: &Path, owner: Option<&Owner>, existed: bool, checking: bool) -> Result<(), String> {
    let path = dir.join("store.sqlite");
    if checking && !existed { return Ok(()); }
    if let Some(owner) = owner {
        let store = owner.open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        match store.meta(KEY).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_deref() {
            Some("staging") => Ok(()),
            None if !existed && !checking => store.set_meta(KEY, "staging").map_err(|e|e.to_string()),
            _ => Err(production()),
        }
    } else {
        let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&path).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        if store.meta(KEY).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_deref() == Some("staging") { Ok(()) } else { Err(production()) }
    }
}

export { loadFixtures, type FixtureRow, type LoadFixturesOptions } from "./proof-fixtures.ts";

// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#1 — the packaged proof kit owns hosts; builders own assertions.
import { mkdirSync, openSync, closeSync, writeSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { resolve } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { createRequire } from "node:module";
import type { BrowserContext, BrowserType, LaunchOptions, Locator, Page } from "playwright-core";
import { resolveBinary } from "./bin-path.ts";
import { bounded, deadline, logged, logText, portRange, ready, stopChild, type ProofHost, type ProofOptions, logsDir } from "./proof-process.ts";
export { bounded, cleanup, stopChild, portRange } from "./proof-process.ts";
export type { ProofHost, ProofOptions } from "./proof-process.ts";

const fetchIdentity = async (url: string, timeoutMs: number) => {
  const response = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs) });
  return { response, body: await response.json() as Record<string, unknown> };
};
async function nativeHost(verb: "dev" | "serve", prefix: string[], options: ProofOptions): Promise<ProofHost & { personas?: string[] }> {
  const env = { ...process.env, ...options.env }, id = randomUUID();
  const ports = portRange(env), timeout = deadline(options.timeoutMs, 30_000);
  const args = [...(options.args ?? [])];
  if (!args.includes("--port")) args.push("--port", ports);
  const run = logged(resolveBinary(), [verb, ...prefix, ...args], { ...options, env: { ...env, SNAPBACK4_PROOF_ID: id, SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID: String(process.pid) } });
  try {
    const url = await ready(run, line => {
      const match = line.match(/^SNAPBACK4_READY verb=(dev|serve) generation=\d+ url=(http:\/\/[^ ]+) pid=(\d+)(?: |$)/);
      if (!match) return;
      if (match[1] !== verb || Number(match[3]) !== run.child.pid) throw Error("proof ready identity mismatch");
      return match[2];
    }, timeout);
    const { response, body } = await fetchIdentity(`${url}/auth/config`, timeout);
    if (!response.ok || body.proof_id !== id) throw Error("proof served identity mismatch");
    let personas: string[] = [];
    if (verb === "dev" && body.devDoors !== false) {
      const status = await fetchIdentity(`${url}/dev/status`, timeout);
      if (status.body.pid !== run.child.pid) throw Error("proof dev PID mismatch");
      if (!Array.isArray(status.body.personas) || !status.body.personas.every(name => typeof name === "string")) throw Error("proof dev personas missing");
      personas = status.body.personas;
    }
    return { url, ...(verb === "dev" ? { personas } : {}), pid: run.child.pid!, logs: run.logs, stop: () => stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)) };
  } catch (error) { await stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)); throw error; }
}
export interface DevHost extends ProofHost {
  personas: string[];
  /** Read the current authoritative development time, including all clock advances. */
  now(): Promise<number>;
}
/** The array form preserves the former startDev(args) recipe. */
export function startDev(options: ProofOptions | readonly string[] = {}): Promise<DevHost> {
  const opts: ProofOptions = Array.isArray(options) ? { args: options } : options as ProofOptions;
  const args = [...(opts.args ?? [])];
  const persistent = args.some(arg => arg === "--store" || arg.startsWith("--store=") || arg === "--no-memory");
  if (persistent && args.includes("--memory")) throw new TypeError("proof startDev: --memory conflicts with --store/--no-memory");
  // Native persistence is the default. --no-memory remains a consumed explicit alias.
  const host = nativeHost("dev", [], { ...opts, args: args.filter(arg => arg !== "--no-memory") });
  return host.then(host => ({ ...host, personas: host.personas!, now: async () => {
    const { response, body } = await fetchIdentity(`${host.url}/dev/status`, deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 10_000));
    if (!response.ok || typeof body.now !== "number") throw Error("proof dev clock unavailable; password-only dev requires explicit --unsafe-dev-doors for clock/fixture tools; use serve staging for real users");
    return body.now;
  } }));
}
export interface ServeHost extends ProofHost {
  operatorToken(service?: string, days?: number): Promise<string>;
  seedAccounts(options: SeedAccountsOptions): Promise<StagingSessions>;
  sessions?: StagingSessions;
}
export interface ServeOptions extends ProofOptions { seedUsers?: SeedAccountsOptions }
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#2-real-staging-accounts — operator authority stays in the existing local store proof; bulk setup refuses production.
export async function startServe(deployDir: string, options: ServeOptions | readonly string[] = {}): Promise<ServeHost> {
  if (typeof deployDir !== "string" || !deployDir.trim()) throw Object.assign(new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: startServe(deployDir, options) requires a deployment directory as its first argument"), { code: "E_PROOF_USAGE" });
  const opts: ServeOptions = Array.isArray(options) ? { args: options } : options as ServeOptions;
  const directory = resolve(opts.cwd ?? ".", deployDir);
  const host = await nativeHost("serve", [directory], opts);
  const served: ServeHost = { ...host,
    operatorToken: async (service = "operator", days = 1) => {
      if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(service) || !Number.isSafeInteger(days) || days < 1 || days > 3650) throw Error("proof operatorToken: invalid service or days (1..3650)");
      const result = await cli(["token", "--service", service, "--days", String(days), "--release", directory, "--quiet"], opts);
      if (result.code !== 0) throw Error(`proof operator token refused: ${result.stderr}`);
      const token = result.stdout.trim();
      if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/.test(token)) throw Error("proof operator token malformed");
      return token;
    },
    seedAccounts: accountOptions => seedAccounts(served, accountOptions),
  };
  try { if (opts.seedUsers) served.sessions = await served.seedAccounts(opts.seedUsers); return served; }
  catch (error) { await served.stop(); throw error; }
}
async function frontend(kind: "static" | "vite", dir: string, options: ProofOptions): Promise<ProofHost> {
  const env = { ...process.env, ...options.env }, ports = portRange(env), timeout = deadline(options.timeoutMs, 30_000);
  const cwd = resolve(options.cwd ?? ".");
  const directory = resolve(cwd, dir);
  // Vite is resolved from the app, never the proof package's development dependencies.
  const vite = kind === "vite" ? createRequire(resolve(directory, "package.json")).resolve("vite") : "";
  const run = logged(process.execPath, [fileURLToPath(new URL("./proof-host.js", import.meta.url)), kind, directory, ports, vite], { ...options, env, detached: process.platform !== "win32", livenessPipe: true });
  try {
    const url = await ready(run, line => {
      const match = line.match(/^SNAPBACK4_PROOF_READY (\{.*\})$/);
      if (!match) return;
      const data = JSON.parse(match[1]!);
      if (data.pid !== run.child.pid || data.kind !== kind) throw Error("proof frontend PID mismatch");
      return data.url as string;
    }, timeout);
    const response = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeout) });
    if (response.headers.get("x-snapback4-proof") !== String(run.child.pid) + ":" + run.id) throw Error("proof frontend identity mismatch");
    await response.body?.cancel();
    return { url, pid: run.child.pid!, logs: run.logs, stop: () => stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)) };
  } catch (error) { await stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)); throw error; }
}
export function startStatic(dir: string, options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<ProofHost> { return frontend("static", dir, options); }
export function startVite(dir: string, options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<ProofHost> { return frontend("vite", dir, options); }

export interface CliResult { code: number | null; signal: NodeJS.Signals | null; stdout: string; stderr: string; logs: ProofHost["logs"] }
/** Waits for close (stdio drained), never merges the two streams. Nonzero is returned for refusal assertions. */
export function cli(args: readonly string[], options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<CliResult> {
  return command(process.execPath, [fileURLToPath(new URL("../bin/snapback4.js", import.meta.url)), ...args], { ...options, secret: options.secret || args[0] === "token" });
}
/** A bounded directly spawned build/tool command, with the same file logs. */
export async function command(binary: string, args: readonly string[], options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<CliResult> {
  const run = logged(binary, args, { ...options, env: { ...process.env, ...options.env } });
  try {
    await bounded(run.closed, deadline(options.timeoutMs, 120_000), "proof cli close");
    if (run.error()) throw run.error();
    return { code: run.child.exitCode, signal: run.child.signalCode, stdout: run.stdout(), stderr: logText(run.logs.stderr), logs: run.logs };
  } catch (error) { await stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)); throw error; }
  finally { run.child.stdout?.destroy(); } // Secret capture must not pin the driver after a deadline.
}
async function successful(args: string[], options: ProofOptions) {
  const result = await cli(args, options);
  if (result.code !== 0) {
    const stdout = result.stdout.trimEnd();
    const tail = stdout.length > 4000 ? `…${stdout.slice(-4000)}` : stdout;
    throw Error(`proof CLI failed (${result.code}): ${result.stderr}; stdout tail: ${tail || "(empty)"}; logs: ${result.logs.stderr}`);
  }
  return result;
}
export function deploy(dir: string, schema: string, options: ProofOptions = {}) {
  if (typeof schema !== "string" || !schema) throw Error(`proof deploy requires an explicit saved schema file or directory; got ${JSON.stringify(schema) ?? String(schema)}: did npm run day2:proof pass versions/v1?`);
  return successful(["deploy", dir, "--schema", schema, ...(options.args ?? [])], options);
}
export function rollback(dir: string, to: number | "latest", options: ProofOptions = {}) {
  if (to !== "latest" && (!Number.isSafeInteger(to) || to < 1)) throw Error(`proof rollback requires an explicit immutable release target; got ${JSON.stringify(to) ?? String(to)}`);
  return successful(["rollback", dir, "--to", String(to), ...(options.args ?? [])], options);
}
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#11-wave-14-truthful-starter-custody-day4 — saved-source proof results expose authored cases separately from query sweeps.
export interface TestSourceOptions extends ProofOptions { fixtureOnly?: boolean }
export interface TestSourceResult extends CliResult { casesRun: number; casesSkipped: number }
export function testSource(source: string, options: TestSourceOptions = {}): Promise<TestSourceResult> {
  if (typeof source !== "string" || !source) throw Error(`proof testSource requires an explicit saved source file or directory; got ${JSON.stringify(source) ?? String(source)}: pass versions/v1`);
  const { fixtureOnly = false, ...proofOptions } = options;
  return successful(["test", "--source", source, ...(proofOptions.args ?? [])], proofOptions).then(result => {
    const summaries = [...result.stdout.matchAll(/^SNAPBACK4_TEST_CASES (\{.*\})$/gm)];
    if (summaries.length !== 1) throw Error(`proof testSource received no unique business-case summary; stdout tail: ${result.stdout.trimEnd().slice(-4000) || "(empty)"}; logs: ${result.logs.stdout}`);
    let cases: { run: number; skipped: number };
    try { cases = JSON.parse(summaries[0]![1]!) as { run: number; skipped: number }; }
    catch { throw Error(`proof testSource received a malformed business-case summary: ${summaries[0]![0]}; logs: ${result.logs.stdout}`); }
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(cases.run) || cases.run < 0 || !Number.isSafeInteger(cases.skipped) || cases.skipped < 0) {
      throw Error(`proof testSource received invalid business-case counts: ${summaries[0]![0]}; logs: ${result.logs.stdout}`);
    }
    if (cases.run === 0 && !fixtureOnly) {
      throw Error(`proof testSource ran no business cases (${cases.skipped} skipped); add test.cases.jsonl for this saved version, or pass { fixtureOnly: true } when fixture loading alone is the intended proof; stdout tail: ${result.stdout.trimEnd().slice(-4000) || "(empty)"}`);
    }
    return { ...result, casesRun: cases.run, casesSkipped: cases.skipped };
  });
}

import { randomUUID, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import type { ServeHost } from "./proof.ts";

export interface StagingAccount {
  role: string; email: string; password: string; principal: string; token: string; expiresAt: number;
}
export type StagingSessions = Record<string, StagingAccount[]>;
export interface SeedAccountsOptions {
  roles: Record<string, number>;
  provision: { readonly name: string };
  args?: (account: StagingAccount) => Record<string, unknown>;
  /** Whole setup deadline, including rate-limit waits. Default 5 minutes. */
  timeoutMs?: number;
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#2-real-staging-accounts — real credentials and ordinary operator mutations, never system seeding or auth bypass.
export async function seedAccounts(host: Pick<ServeHost, "url" | "operatorToken">, options: SeedAccountsOptions): Promise<StagingSessions> {
  const roles = Object.entries(options.roles), timeout = options.timeoutMs ?? 300_000;
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(timeout) || timeout < 1 || !roles.length || roles.some(([role, n]) => !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}$/.test(role) || !Number.isSafeInteger(n) || n < 1)
      || roles.reduce((sum, [, n]) => sum + n, 0) > 100 || !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*(?:\.[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*)*$/.test(options.provision?.name ?? "")) {
    throw Error("E_STAGING_INPUT: provide 1..100 accounts across named roles, a mutation handle and a positive timeoutMs");
  }
  const end = performance.now() + timeout;
  const remaining = () => {
    const ms = Math.ceil(end - performance.now());
    if (ms <= 0) throw Error("E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: setup incomplete; partial accounts/grants may exist; inspect or discard this staging deployment");
    return ms;
  };
  const request = (path: string, init?: RequestInit) => fetch(host.url + path, { ...init, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(Math.min(remaining(), 30_000)) });
  const config = await request("/auth/config");
  const identity = await config.json();
  if (!config.ok || identity.environment !== "staging" || identity.development !== false || !identity.password) {
    throw Error("E_STAGING: account seeding requires serve on a fresh deploy --staging with password identity; production and dev refuse before creating users");
  }
  const operator = await host.operatorToken();
  const sessions: StagingSessions = Object.create(null);
  for (const [role, count] of roles) {
    sessions[role] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const email = `staging-${role}-${randomUUID()}@example.test`, password = randomBytes(24).toString("base64url");
      let reply;
      // Only an explicit HTTP 429 refusal authorizes retrying signup. Network
      // failures and malformed/ambiguous successful replies are never retried.
      for (;;) {
        const response = await request("/auth/signup", { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }) });
        reply = await response.json();
        if (response.status !== 429 || reply.denied?.code !== "E_RATE_LIMIT") break;
        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, Math.min(1000, remaining())));
      }
      const session = reply.session;
      if (typeof session?.principal !== "string" || typeof session.token !== "string" || typeof session.expiresAt !== "number") {
        throw Error(`E_STAGING_SIGNUP: signup refused (${reply.denied?.code ?? "invalid reply"}); partial accounts/grants may exist`);
      }
      const account: StagingAccount = { role, email, password, principal: session.principal, token: session.token, expiresAt: session.expiresAt };
      const args = options.args ? options.args(account) : { principal: account.principal, role };
      const response = await request(`/m/${options.provision.name}`, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json", authorization: `Bearer ${operator}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ id: randomUUID(), args }) });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok || result.state !== "sent") throw Error(`E_STAGING_PROVISION: ${options.provision.name} refused (${result.why?.code ?? result.denied?.code ?? "invalid reply"}); partial accounts/grants may exist`);
      sessions[role]!.push(account);
    }
  }
  return sessions;
}

fn activate(
    dir: &Path,
    owner: Option<&Owner>,
    release: u64,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    revision: u64,
    bound: snapback4_lang::Bound,
    rollback: bool,
    restore_dormant: bool,
    allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    options: &Options,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let root = dir.join("releases").join(release.to_string());
    let digest = crate::release_files::digest(&root)?;
    if let Some(owner) = owner {
        let project = Project { root };
        let prepared = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project, &bound.schema)?;
        crate::release_files::link_store(&project.root)?;
        crate::release_files::durable(&project.root)?;
        let mut pointer = crate::release_files::Pointer::prepare(dir, release, previous, rollback)?;
        let activation = snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation {
            release,
            expected_previous: previous,
            expected_revision: Some(revision),
            restore_dormant,
            native_digest: prepared.installed.as_ref().map(|m| m.digest.clone()),
            operation: if rollback { "rollback" } else { "deploy" }.into(),
        };
        let store = owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let engine = snapback4_core::Engine::new_release_prepared(
            store,
            bound.schema,
            bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program),
            &activation,
            allow_feed_adoption,
        )
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        pointer.finish(engine.generation())?;
        receipt(release,engine.generation(),&engine.store().adoption_report,options);

    } else {
        let result = crate::release_control::request(
            dir,
            crate::release_control::Command::Activate {
                release,
                expected_release: previous,
                expected_revision: revision,
                digest,
                rollback,
                restore_dormant,
                allow_feed_adoption,
            },
        )?;
        if options.json {println!("{}",result);} else {
            println!("release {release} is current as generation {}; {}; running serve adopted the release",result["generation"],result["adoption"].as_str().unwrap_or("adopted"));
            for fill in result["backfills"].as_array().into_iter().flatten().filter_map(|f|f.as_str()) {println!("  backfill: {fill}");}
            if options.flags.contains_key("verbose") {println!("{}",result["details"].as_str().unwrap_or(""));}
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn report(verb: &str, result: Result<(), String>) -> i32 {
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — release completion owns no engine/lease; bound the final diagnostic drain.
    // The background writer must get a chance to emit adoption estimates before
    // this short-lived command exits. Broken/stalled output cannot hold exit.
    let _ = snapback4_core::diagnostics::drain(std::time::Duration::from_secs(3));
    match result {
        Ok(()) => 0,
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("{verb}: {error}");
            1
        }
    }
}

 succeeded in 0ms:
use super::*;

/// Who is asking: a bearer session, or (in `dev`, from loopback) a persona.
pub(crate) fn principal_of(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<snapback4_core::identity::Capability, Refusal> {
    principal_with_owner(request, shared, None)
}

fn principal_with_owner(request: &Request, shared: &Shared, owner: Option<&Engine>) -> Result<snapback4_core::identity::Capability, Refusal> {
    // Declared public bearer routes keep their existing development admission.
    if !(request.method() == &Method::Post && routes::declared(shared, request.url()).is_some()
        && !request.headers().iter().any(|h| h.field.equiv("x-snapback-persona"))) {
        dev_request(request, shared)?;
    }
    let mut persona = None;
    let mut bearer = None;
    for header in request.headers() {
        let name = header.field.as_str().as_str().to_ascii_lowercase();
        let value = header.value.as_str().trim();
        if name == "x-snapback-persona" {
            if persona.is_some()
                || value.is_empty()
                || !value.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_' || c == '-')
            {
                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send exactly one valid x-snapback-persona header").guide("personas"));
            }
            persona = Some(value.to_owned());
        }
        if name == "authorization" {
            let Some(token) = crate::auth::parse_bearer(value) else {
                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send Authorization: Bearer <token>").guide("personas"));
            };
            if bearer.replace(token.to_owned()).is_some() {
                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send exactly one Authorization header").guide("personas"));
            }
        }
    }
    if persona.is_some() && bearer.is_some() {
        return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send one credential: a development persona or a bearer token").guide("personas"));
    }
    if let Some(persona) = persona {
        if let Some(engine) = owner { shared.dev_authority(engine)?; }
        if shared.dev_doors() {
            // `use personas` is the cast: a test expecting an outsider to be
            // refused must not see it signed in. No declaration admits none.
            // (Taken briefly; the engine
            // guard, when held, is always acquired before this one.)
            let declared = lock(&shared.bound).personas.clone();
            if !declared.contains(&persona) {
                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", format!(
                    "persona {persona} is not declared; `use personas {}` is this project's cast. Add {persona} to it (dev re-adopts on save) or send a declared persona", declared.join(", ")))
                    .rewrite(format!("use personas {}{persona}", if declared.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!("{}, ", declared.join(", ")) })).guide("personas"));
            }
            return Ok((persona_principal(&persona), "persona".into(), snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full));
        }
        return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "personas are a development door; a deployed server takes bearer tokens (snapback4/auth)").guide("personas"));
    }
    if let Some(token) = bearer {
        // Reads may authenticate against the committed generation during adoption.
        // Keep write authentication behind the writer: time queued for adoption
        // must not preserve a session that expired or was revoked in that wait.
        let path = request.url().split('?').next().unwrap_or("");
        let read = detached_request(request.method(), path);
        let resolve = |engine: &Engine, identity: Option<&str>, detached: bool| {
            let now = shared.now();
            let sample = snapback4_core::identity::ServiceSample { now, supervisor_revision: shared.worker_revision.load(Ordering::Acquire) };
            let result = if detached {
                snapback4_core::identity::resolve_read_only_supervised(engine.store(), &token, sample, identity)
            } else {
                snapback4_core::identity::resolve_supervised(engine.store(), &token, sample)
            };
            let result=result.map_err(|error| error.refusal()).and_then(|value|value.ok_or_else(||
                snapback4_core::identity::diagnostics::refused(engine.store(), &token, now, trusted_dev_request(request, shared))));
            if result.is_err() {crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());}
            result
        };
        return if let Some(engine) = owner { resolve(engine, None, false) }
        else if read { match shared.reader() {
            Ok((epoch, reader)) => {
                let identity = lock(&shared.wake).store_id.clone();
                let result = resolve(&reader, identity.as_deref(), true);
                lock(&shared.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
                result
            }
            Err(_) if shared.memory_store() => resolve(&lock(&shared.engine), None, false),
            Err(error) => Err(error.refusal()),
        } } else { resolve(&lock(&shared.engine), None, false) };
    }
    Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "sign in: send a bearer token (snapback4/auth), or x-snapback-persona in development").guide("personas"))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#4-development-doors — a persona cannot cross from authentication into a replacement password-only reader.
pub(crate) fn persona_read_authority(request: &Request, shared: &Shared, engine: &Engine) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
    let Some(persona) = request.headers().iter().find(|header| header.field.equiv("x-snapback-persona")) else { return Ok(()) };
    shared.dev_authority(engine)?;
    let bound = lock(&shared.bound);
    if (!shared.unsafe_dev_doors && bound.schema.auth.password && !bound.schema.auth.guests)
        || !bound.personas.iter().any(|name| name == persona.value.as_str().trim()) {
        return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "persona authority changed while this read waited; sign in with a password or use the current declared development cast").guide("staging"));
    }
    // The cast is checked at this read's admission. Never hold its publication
    // mutex during I/O: concurrent ordinary writes still need to authenticate.
    // The engine transaction fences a replaced execution schema.
    Ok(())
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — adoption's writer queue cannot retain expired bearer authority.
pub(super) fn mutation_authority(request: &Request, shared: &Shared, engine: &Engine, principal: &str) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
    if principal_with_owner(request, shared, Some(engine))?.0 == principal { Ok(()) }
    else { Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "the request's session changed; sign in again").guide("personas")) }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.033#1.2 — additions require an explicit decision here.
pub(super) fn read_permitted(method: &Method, path: &str) -> bool {
    match method {
        Method::Get => matches!(path, "/changes" | "/probe" | "/schema") || path.starts_with("/assets/"),
        Method::Post => matches!(path, "/sync" | "/probe" | "/auth/refresh" | "/auth/logout") || path.starts_with("/q/"),
        _ => false,
    }
}

# Staging with real users

Use `serve` for staging, shared previews and `probe:serve`. It enforces the same
password identity and rules as production. `dev` always advertises
`development:true` in `/auth/config`, even with closed doors; probes should
refuse it. `guide deploy` covers release evolution; PROD2 owns starter adaptation.

## Password roles and operator provisioning

Save this complete source as `snapback/schema.q` in an app with snapback4 installed.
Signup creates an account without a role. Only the local filesystem operator can
bootstrap this roster. The insert rule enforces the boundary independently of
the mutation. SEC owns self-promotion diagnostics and invariant proofs.

```quarry
use identity password
table roles:
  principal: principal
  role: enum('admin', 'member')
  unique principal
  immutable principal
  read <- .principal = viewer or viewer = 'service:operator'
  insert <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  update <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  online only
mutation provision(principal: principal, role: enum('admin', 'member')):
  require viewer = 'service:operator' else NOT_OPERATOR
  return upsert roles[principal] { principal, role }
query myRole():
  return roles[viewer]
table notes:
  owner: principal
  body: text 1..200
  read <- .owner = viewer
  insert <- .owner = viewer
  online only
mutation add(body: text 1..200):
  return insert notes { owner: viewer, body }
query myNotes():
  return notes where .owner = viewer first 20 by .id
```

## Stand up staging

Run `npx snapback4 check` to generate the API. Save this as `staging.mjs` next to
snapback/, then run `node staging.mjs`. Use `node staging.mjs --once` for CI setup
and shutdown. The deployment and private credentials file remain for inspection;
choose fresh names for each independent run. Add both to .gitignore. PORT_LO and
PORT_HI optionally choose a port range. The printed URL belongs to `serve`.

```js
import { openSync, writeFileSync, closeSync } from "node:fs";
import { deploy, startServe } from "snapback4/proof";
import { api } from "./snapback/generated/api.ts";

await deploy("staging", "snapback", { args: ["--staging"] });
const host = await startServe("staging", { seedUsers: {
  roles: { admin: 2, member: 2 }, provision: api.provision,
} });
try {
  // This file contains real passwords and bearer tokens. Never commit it.
  const fd = openSync("staging-users.json", "wx", 0o600);
  try { writeFileSync(fd, JSON.stringify(host.sessions)); } finally { closeSync(fd); }
  for (const accounts of Object.values(host.sessions)) {
    for (const account of accounts) {
      const response = await fetch(host.url + "/m/add", {
        method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json", authorization: `Bearer ${account.token}` },
        body: JSON.stringify({ args: { body: `Welcome ${account.role}` } }),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10000),
      });
      if ((await response.json()).state !== "sent") throw Error("staging data write refused");
    }
  }
  console.log(JSON.stringify({ backendUrl: host.url, credentialsFile: "staging-users.json" }));
  if (!process.argv.includes("--once")) await new Promise(resolve => {
    process.once("SIGTERM", resolve); process.once("SIGINT", resolve);
  });
} finally { await host.stop(); }
```

`host.seedAccounts({roles, provision, args?})` performs additional batches; an
optional `args(account)` maps principal/role/email to your mutation's arguments.
Each call creates new random email/password pairs and returns role arrays of
`{role,email,password,principal,token,expiresAt}`. Ordinary sessions last seven
days; sign in again with `login` from snapback4/auth when needed. Setup is
sequential and bounded (default five minutes, timeoutMs overrides it, max 100
accounts). Only explicit identity throttling refusals retry; ambiguous writes
do not. Partial failure can leave accounts/grants: inspect or discard this
disposable deployment before retrying. Returned credentials never enter helper
logs. Print them only to a private operator destination, as above.

## Production refuses bulk setup

`deploy --staging` can classify only a new store. Existing unmarked stores are
production, and refuse relabelling. Classification stays with the store across
hot deploys, rollbacks and restarts; copying production into a directory named
staging does not change it. `deploy --check --staging` does not mark a store.
There is no promotion flag: deploy real production into its own fresh directory
and provision only intended users. `seedAccounts` and startServe's seedUsers
refuse production or dev before minting a token or creating any account. A
normal production startServe still supports operatorToken for explicit grants.

### E_STAGING

E_STAGING refuses production relabelling or bulk account setup on a non-staging
host. Choose a separate fresh `deploy --staging` store; use the explicit
first-admin recipe below for intended production users. Source edits and a
directory named staging never change a production store's classification.

## First admin on production

Deploy the same schema with `npx snapback4 deploy production` (omit --staging).
Save the following as `first-admin.mjs`. Set FIRST_ADMIN_EMAIL and
FIRST_ADMIN_PASSWORD privately, then run `node first-admin.mjs` as the store
owner while no other serve owns its port. This script starts and stops serve;
restart it normally afterwards. For an already running deployment use
`snapback4 token --service operator --days 1 --release production` locally and
the same authenticated mutation. Keep the service token out of the frontend.

```js
import { signup, login } from "snapback4/auth";
import { startServe } from "snapback4/proof";
const email = process.env.FIRST_ADMIN_EMAIL, password = process.env.FIRST_ADMIN_PASSWORD;
if (!email || !password) throw Error("Set FIRST_ADMIN_EMAIL and FIRST_ADMIN_PASSWORD privately");
const host = await startServe("production");
try {
  // Set FIRST_ADMIN_EXISTING=1 only for an account you already created.
  const account = await (process.env.FIRST_ADMIN_EXISTING === "1" ? login : signup)(host.url, email, password);
  if (!account.ok) throw Error(account.why.message);
  const token = await host.operatorToken();
  const response = await fetch(host.url + "/m/provision", {
    method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json", authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
    body: JSON.stringify({ args: { principal: account.session.principal, role: "admin" } }),
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10000),
  });
  if ((await response.json()).state !== "sent") throw Error("operator grant refused");
  console.log("First admin provisioned; sign in with the chosen email and password");
} finally { await host.stop(); }
```

No first-user race or self-promotion mutation is needed. Subsequent grants use
the same operator process or an explicitly authorized admin policy. Invite codes
must be server-validated, single-use grants issued by an authorized operator;
an arbitrary caller-supplied code or role is not authority.

## Local fixture tools are explicit

Password-only dev closes /seed, /dev/* and persona headers by default.
`dev --unsafe-dev-doors` explicitly opens local fixture and clock tools and
prints a warning; it still requires loopback Host/Origin, and personas must be
declared with `use personas`. The local token-mint protocol keeps its existing
private store-owner proof. Serve never opens these development tools.
`/auth/config` reports `environment`, `development`, and `devDoors` separately.
`startDev()` persists by default, matching native dev; pass args:["--memory"]
for disposable storage. now() needs the explicit clock door on password-only
dev. A proof for real users uses the serve recipe above.

+// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#4-development-doors — a persona cannot cross from authentication into a replacement password-only reader.
+pub(crate) fn persona_read_authority(request: &Request, shared: &Shared, engine: &Engine) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
+    let Some(persona) = request.headers().iter().find(|header| header.field.equiv("x-snapback-persona")) else { return Ok(()) };
+    shared.dev_authority(engine)?;
+    let bound = lock(&shared.bound);
+    if (!shared.unsafe_dev_doors && bound.schema.auth.password && !bound.schema.auth.guests)
+        || !bound.personas.iter().any(|name| name == persona.value.as_str().trim()) {
+        return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "persona authority changed while this read waited; sign in with a password or use the current declared development cast").guide("staging"));
+    }
+    // The cast is checked at this read's admission. Never hold its publication
+    // mutex during I/O: concurrent ordinary writes still need to authenticate.
+    // The engine transaction fences a replaced execution schema.
+    Ok(())

 succeeded in 0ms:
    // `into_writer` drops the request reader before it returns. tiny_http
    // prebuffers ordinary bodies up to 1 KiB; other readers may drain on
    // drop, so keep its response-before-drain order for those requests.
    let may_drain = request.body_length().is_some_and(|n| n > 1024) || request.headers().iter().any(|h| {
        h.field.equiv("Expect") || h.field.equiv("Transfer-Encoding")
            || (h.field.equiv("Connection") && h.value.as_str().split(',').any(|v| v.trim().eq_ignore_ascii_case("upgrade")))
    });
    if may_drain {
        let _ = request.respond_checked_with_completion(response, |result| latency::finished(result.is_err() || refused));
        return;
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — coalesce buffered JSON HTTP responses
    // The body is already materialized. Frame it before writing so the HTTP
    // library's small socket buffer cannot split headers from a short body
    // and leave the latter waiting for a delayed TCP acknowledgement.
    let mut encoded = Vec::with_capacity(capacity);
    let response = request.prepare_response(response);
    if response.raw_print(&mut encoded, request.http_version().clone(), request.headers(), request.method() == &Method::Head, None).is_ok() {
        let mut writer = request.into_writer();
        trace::mark("write_start");
        latency::finished(writer.write_all(&encoded).and_then(|_| writer.flush()).is_err() || refused);
    }
}

/// A 204 has no representation metadata or advertised bytes. Keeping it out
/// of the JSON response helper also prevents an unread `null` from framing the
/// next response on a persistent connection.
pub(crate) fn respond_empty(request: Request, status: u16) {
    let mut response = Response::empty(status);
    if let Some(header)=generation_header(){response.add_header(header);}
    for header in cors(&request).into_iter().chain(latency::header()).filter(|header| !header.field.equiv("Content-Type")) {
        response.add_header(header);
    }
    let _ = request.respond(response);
}

pub(crate) fn refusal_json(refusal: &Refusal) -> Json {
    serde_json::to_value(refusal).unwrap_or(Json::Null)
}


pub(crate) fn query_params(url: &str) -> BTreeMap<String, String> {
    fn decode(text: &str) -> String {
        let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(text.len());
        let bytes = text.as_bytes();
        let mut at = 0;
        while at < bytes.len() {
            if bytes[at] == b'%' && at + 2 < bytes.len() {
                let hex = |b: u8| (b as char).to_digit(16).map(|n| n as u8);
                if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (hex(bytes[at+1]), hex(bytes[at+2])) { out.push(a * 16 + b); at += 3; continue; }
            }
            out.push(if bytes[at] == b'+' { b' ' } else { bytes[at] }); at += 1;
        }
        String::from_utf8(out).unwrap_or_else(|_| text.to_owned())
    }
    url.split_once('?')
        .map(|(_, query)| query.split('&').filter_map(|pair| pair.split_once('=').map(|(k, v)| (decode(k), decode(v)))).collect())
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

// Identity and head are a single snapshot from the owner of the evaluated
// operation. Mutation replay's seq is historical; head is never that receipt.
fn response_head(engine: &mut Engine) -> Result<(String, u64), Refusal> {
    let tx = engine.store_mut().read_transaction()?;
    let id: String = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'store_id'", [], |row| row.get(0)).map_err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::from)?;
    Ok((id, tx.next_seq()? - 1))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.013#6 — a replay belongs to its acknowledged store, even if
// the endpoint changes between the client's probe and this transaction.
fn intent_store(engine: &Engine, payload: &Json) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
    let Some(expected) = payload.get("store_id").filter(|id| !id.is_null()) else { return Ok(()); };
    let current = engine.store().meta("store_id")?;
    if expected.as_str().is_some_and(|id| Some(id) == current.as_deref()) { return Ok(()); }
    Err(Refusal::new("E_STORE_REPLACED", "store", "the intent belongs to another store; its original outcome is unknown")
        .site("request.store_id").rewrite("reconcile this write with its original store before submitting new intent").guide("sync"))
}

fn operation_refusal(request: Request, shared: &Shared, status: u16, why: Refusal) {
    let metadata = shared.read_identity();
    match metadata {
        Ok((store_id, head, generation)) => respond_timed(request, shared, status, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&why), "store_id": store_id, "head": head, "generation": generation })),
        Err(why) => respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&why) })),
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.009#5.1 — a racing id conflict is a terminal input refusal, not a transport failure.
fn mutation_error_status(refusal: &Refusal) -> u16 {
    if refusal.retryable { 503 } else if matches!(refusal.code.as_str(), "E_ID_REUSED" | "E_DELIVERY_BINDING") { 200 } else { 500 }
}

fn handle(mut request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#93-http-admission-and-reporting — lookup, decode and execution share the admitted release.
    let operation = request.url().split('?').next().is_some_and(|p| p.starts_with("/q/") || p.starts_with("/m/"));
    let _scope=crate::release_requests::Scope::begin(*lock(&shared.generation));

    request.set_cleanup_guard(latency::FinishOnDrop);
    #[cfg(test)] test_work(&shared.request_work);
    let method = request.method().clone();

    let _evidence = dev_refusals::response::begin(shared, &path);
    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.2 — closed read allow-list precedes every handler,
    // declared route, dev door and idempotency replay. Refusals carry no head.
    // Read tokens and malformed credentials always enter this guard. Otherwise
    // authenticated full-token handlers retain their input/refusal ordering.
    // Public and dev doors must resolve any supplied credential before dispatch.
    let public_door = path.starts_with("/dev/") || matches!(path.as_str(), "/seed" | "/auth/signup" | "/auth/login" | "/auth/guest")
        || path == token::ENDPOINT || routes::declared(shared, &url).is_some();
    if request.headers().iter().any(|h| h.field.equiv("authorization")
        && (public_door || crate::auth::parse_bearer(h.value.as_str()).is_none_or(|token| token.starts_with("ro.")))) {
        match principal_of(&request, shared) {
            Ok((_, _, snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Read)) if !read_permitted(&method, &path) =>
                return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied": Refusal::new("E_READ_ONLY", "auth", "this read session permits reads only; use a full session for writes").guide("read-only")})),
            // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — the private-proof door keeps one indistinguishable refusal.
            Err(_) if path == token::ENDPOINT => return token::denied(request),
            Err(why) => return respond_timed(request, shared, if why.retryable {503} else {401}, json!({"denied": refusal_json(&why)})),
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    if path == token::ENDPOINT { return token::handle(request, shared); }
    // This exact declared POST path is the only non-loopback development
    // exception. Core doors, mismatched methods and query variants stay fenced.
    if method == Method::Post && let Some((name, route)) = routes::declared(shared, &url) {
        return routes::ingress(request, shared, &name, route);
    }

    let reject = |request, status, refusal: Refusal| {
        if ephemeral_request { ephemeral::failed(request,shared,None,refusal); }
        else if path.starts_with("/q/") { operation_refusal(request, shared, status, refusal); }
        else { respond_timed(request,shared,status,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal)})); }
    };
    if let Err(refusal) = dev_request(&request, shared) {
        if ephemeral_request { return ephemeral::failed(request, shared, None, refusal); }
        respond_with_sync(request, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }), false, None, Some(shared));
        return;
    }
    // /dev/token has already been routed through its mutual local-owner proof.
    if shared.personas && (path.starts_with("/dev/") || path == "/seed") && !shared.dev_doors() {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&dev_doors::closed())}));
    }
    if path.starts_with("/dev/clock") && !shared.personas {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::release()) }));
    }
    // Discovered control requests bind to the inspected serving instance.
    // A listener replaced between GET status and POST cannot receive the action.
    if method==Method::Post && matches!(path.as_str(), "/dev/clock/advance"|"/dev/reset"|"/dev/latency/reset") {
        match single_header(&request,"x-snapback-dev-instance") {
            Ok(None)=>{},
            Ok(Some(instance)) if shared.dev_identity.as_ref().is_some_and(|i| i.instance==instance)=>{},
            _=>return respond_timed(request,shared,409,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_INPUT","input","dev instance changed since discovery; rerun the command to select the live host"))})),
        }
    }
    if (method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/latency") || (method == Method::Post && path == "/dev/latency/reset") {
        return latency::endpoint(request, shared, method == Method::Post);
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#3-proof-fixture-loading — development field types need no principal.
    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/catalog" {

        let tables: Vec<Json> = bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, table)| json!({ "name": name, "fields": table.columns.iter().filter(|(column, _)| !table.private_field(column) && !table.engine_field(column)).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>() })).collect();
        drop(bound);
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({ "tables": tables }));
    }
    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/status" {
        if !shared.personas { return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "status inspection requires dev"))})); }
        // No engine lock: doctor must remain usable while an adoption waits on SQLite.

        let personas = lock(&shared.bound).personas.clone();

    }
    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/clock/status" { return maintenance::clock_status(request, shared); }
    if method == Method::Options {
        respond_empty(request, 204);

    }
    if method == Method::Post && path == "/seed" && !shared.personas {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "seeding is a development door")) }));
    }
    if path == "/dev/reset" && !shared.personas {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&dev_doors::reset_release()) }));
    }
    // Check identity before upload admission or allocating any large body.
    // The route rechecks after reading, so expiry/revocation during a slow

    // Logout's body is only 4 KiB and its sole authentication happens inside

    if method == Method::Post && (path == "/assets" || path.starts_with("/assets/intent/") || path == "/sync" || path.starts_with("/q/") || path.starts_with("/following/") || path.starts_with("/m/") || path.starts_with("/a/")

    if method == Method::Post && (path == "/auth/revoke" || path.starts_with("/routes/verify/") || (path.starts_with("/actions/") && !path.ends_with("/cancel"))) {
        if let Err(refusal) = service_of(&request, shared) {
            return reject(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, refusal);
        }
    }
    if method == Method::Get && matches!(path.as_str(), "/" | "/schema" | "/api.ts" | "/native.wasm" | "/changes") {
        if let Err(refusal) = principal_of(&request, shared) {
            return reject(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, refusal);
        }
    }
    let _upload_permit = if method == Method::Post && path == "/assets" {
        match crate::assets::admit(shared) {
            Some(permit) => Some(permit),
            None => {
                let mut refusal = Refusal::new("E_RATE_LIMIT", "auth", "too many concurrent uploads; retry shortly");
                refusal.retryable = true;
                return respond_timed(request, shared, 429, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }));
            }
        }
    } else { None };
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    // @ref LLP 3000.038#3 — a file upload streams at most the schema's largest file bound, refused before reading when declared larger.
    let file_upload = method == Method::Post && path == "/assets" && crate::assets::is_file_upload(&url);
    if method == Method::Post {
        let ceiling = match path.as_str() {
            "/assets" if file_upload => match crate::assets::file_ceiling(shared) {
                Ok(ceiling) => ceiling,
                Err(refusal) => return reject(request, 415, refusal),
            },
            "/assets" => crate::assets::UPLOAD_CEILING,

            path if path.starts_with("/following/") => 64 * 1024,
            _ => JSON_REQUEST_CEILING,
        };
        let length = request.body_length();

            Refusal::new("E_ASSET_TOO_LARGE", "asset", format!("the file exceeds {ceiling} bytes, the largest file column's bound")).guide("assets")
        } else {
            Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("the request body exceeds its {ceiling}-byte limit; split it into smaller requests"))
        };
        if length.is_some_and(|length| length > ceiling) {
            return reject(request, 413, too_large());
        }
        let mut buffer = [0; 8 * 1024];
        loop {
            let length = buffer.len().min(ceiling - bytes.len() + 1);
            match request.as_reader().read(&mut buffer[..length]) {
                Ok(0) => break,
                Ok(read) if read > ceiling - bytes.len() => return reject(request, 413, too_large()),
                Ok(read) => bytes.extend_from_slice(&buffer[..read]),
                Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::Interrupted => continue,
                Err(error) => return reject(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("cannot read the request body: {error}"))),
            }
        }
        if length.is_some_and(|length| length != bytes.len()) {
            return reject(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "the request body ended before its declared Content-Length"));
        }
    }
    if method == Method::Post && path == "/assets" {
        return crate::assets::upload(request, bytes, shared);
    }
    if method == Method::Post && let Some(id)=path.strip_prefix("/assets/intent/").and_then(|s|s.strip_suffix("/declare")){return crate::assets::declare(request,id,bytes,shared);}
    if method == Method::Post && let Some(id) = path.strip_prefix("/assets/intent/").and_then(|s| s.strip_suffix("/finish")) {
        return crate::assets::finish(request, id, bytes, shared);
    }
    if method == Method::Post && let Some(id) = path.strip_prefix("/assets/intent/").and_then(|s| s.strip_suffix("/cancel")) {
        return crate::assets::cancel(request, id, shared);
    }
    if method == Method::Get && path == "/assets/intents" { return crate::assets::intents(request, shared); }
    if method == Method::Get && let Some(id) = path.strip_prefix("/assets/") {
        return crate::assets::serve(request, id, shared);
    }
    let body = match String::from_utf8(bytes) {
        Ok(body) => body,
        Err(_) => return reject(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "a JSON request body must be valid UTF-8")),
    };
    let request_now = if (method == Method::Get && path == "/changes")
        || (method == Method::Post && (path == "/sync" || path.starts_with("/q/") || path.starts_with("/m/")))
    {
        match shared.advance_time_audiences().map_err(|error| error.refusal()) {
            Ok(now) => Some(now),
            Err(refusal) => return reject(request, 503, refusal),
        }
    } else { None };
    if operation && !shared.personas {crate::release_requests::Scope::admit(&shared.release_requests,||*lock(&shared.generation));}
    match (method, path.as_str()) {
        (Method::Post, "/dev/clock/advance") => maintenance::advance(request, shared, &body),
        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/jobs/") => maintenance::run_job(request, shared, &path["/jobs/".len()..], &body),
        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/dev/jobs/") => maintenance::run_job(request, shared, &path["/dev/jobs/".len()..], &body),
        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/a/") => actions::call(request, shared, path.trim_start_matches("/a/"), &body),
        (Method::Post, "/actions/claim") => actions::claim(request, shared, &body),
        (Method::Post, "/actions/claim-set") => actions::claim_set(request, shared, &body),
        (Method::Post, "/routes/verify/claim") => routes::claim(request, shared, &body),
        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/routes/verify/") => routes::settle(request, shared, path, &body),
        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/actions/") => actions::settle(request, shared, path, &body),
        (Method::Post, "/effects/claim") => effects::claim(request, shared, &body),
        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/effects/") => effects::post(request, shared, path, &body),
        (Method::Get, "/effects") => effects::list(request, shared, &url),
        (Method::Get | Method::Post, "/probe") => match principal_of(&request, shared) {
            Ok(viewer) => {
                let identity = shared.read_identity();
                match identity {
                    Ok((store_id, seq,generation)) => {
                        let mut reply = json!({ "ok": true, "store_id": store_id, "seq": seq, "generation":generation,"viewer": viewer.0 });
                        if query_params(&url).get("scope").is_some_and(|scope| scope == "1") {
                            reply["kind"] = json!(viewer.1); reply["scope"] = json!(viewer.2);
                        }
                        respond_timed(request, shared, 200, reply)
                    },
                    Err(error) => respond_timed(request, shared, 500, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&error) })),
                }
            },
            Err(refusal) => respond_timed(request, shared, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) })),
        },
        (Method::Get, "/auth/config") => {
            let (auth, environment) = {
                let engine = producing_engine(shared);
                let environment = if shared.personas { "development" }
                    else if engine.store().meta("deployment_environment").ok().flatten().as_deref() == Some("staging") { "staging" } else { "production" };
                (engine.schema().auth.clone(), environment)

use super::*;

const SOURCE: &str = "use identity\ntable items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  update <- allow\njob mark():\n  for item in items:\n    update items[item] { n: 9 }\n";

fn close_doors(shared: &Shared, engine: &mut Engine) {
    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), SOURCE.replace("use identity", "use identity password"))]);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
    let next = engine.adopt(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone())).unwrap();
    lock(&shared.readers).replace(engine, &bound.refusal_sites);
    *lock(&shared.bound) = bound;
    *lock(&shared.generation) = next;
}

#[test]
fn stage_slow_dev_bodies_cannot_retain_authority_after_password_adoption() {
    for (path, payload) in [("/seed", "[]"), ("/dev/reset", "{}"), ("/dev/clock/advance", r#"{"duration":"+1h"}"#), ("/dev/jobs/mark", "{}")] {
        let host = Host::with_default_doors(SOURCE);
        let before = host.shared.now();
        let mut stream = TcpStream::connect(host.address).unwrap();
        stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(5))).unwrap();
        write!(stream, "POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nExpect: 100-continue\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n", payload.len()).unwrap();
        let mut interim = [0; 128];
        let n = stream.read(&mut interim).unwrap();
        assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&interim[..n]).contains("100 Continue"), "passed the pre-body door fence");
        close_doors(&host.shared, &mut lock(&host.shared.engine));
        stream.write_all(payload.as_bytes()).unwrap();
        let mut response = String::new(); stream.read_to_string(&mut response).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH", "{path}: {response}");
        assert!(host.shared.now() - before < 5000, "refused clock did not advance");
    }
}

#[test]
fn stage_queued_dev_requests_recheck_authority_at_the_writer() {
    for (path, payload) in [("/seed", "[]"), ("/dev/reset", "{}"), ("/dev/clock/advance", r#"{"duration":"+1h"}"#), ("/dev/jobs/mark", "{}")] {
        let host = Host::with_default_doors(SOURCE);
        let mut engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
        let address = host.address;
        let task = thread::spawn(move || exchange_held(address, Duration::from_secs(10), &format!("POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{payload}", payload.len())));
        wait_until(|| host.shared.engine.waiting() > 0);
        close_doors(&host.shared, &mut engine);
        drop(engine);
        let response = task.join().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH", "{path}: {response}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn stage_persona_cannot_acquire_a_replacement_password_only_reader() {
    for file in [false, true] {
        let dir = ReaderTestDirectory::new();
        let store = if file { snapback4_core::Store::open(&dir.0.join("store.sqlite")).unwrap() }
            else { snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap() };
        let source = "use identity\nuse personas alice\nquery secret():\n  return 1\n";
        let host = Host::with_default_doors_store(source, store);
        let (entered, waiting) = mpsc::channel();
        let barrier = Arc::new((Mutex::new(false), Condvar::new()));
        let held = barrier.clone();
        let once = std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool::new(false);
        *lock(&host.shared.reader_open) = Some(Arc::new(move || {
            if once.swap(true, Ordering::AcqRel) { return; }
            entered.send(()).unwrap();
            let (state, wake) = &*held;
            let mut ready = lock(state);
            while !*ready { ready = wake.wait(ready).unwrap(); }
        }));
        let address = host.address;
        let task = thread::spawn(move || exchange_held(address, Duration::from_secs(10),
            "POST /q/secret HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nx-snapback-persona: alice\r\nContent-Length: 11\r\n\r\n{\"args\":{}}"));
        waiting.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(5)).unwrap();
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.replace("use identity", "use identity password").replace("return 1", "return 9876"))]);
        {
            let mut engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
            let generation = engine.adopt(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone())).unwrap();
            lock(&host.shared.readers).replace(&engine, &bound.refusal_sites);
            *lock(&host.shared.bound) = bound;
            *lock(&host.shared.generation) = generation;
        }
        *lock(&barrier.0) = true; barrier.1.notify_all();
        let response = task.join().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(status(&response), 401, "file={file}: {response}");
        assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH");
        assert!(body(&response).get("data").is_none(), "new result never crosses the closed persona door");
    }
}

test("staging accounts are real password users; service grants obey rules; marker survives release switches", { timeout: 90000 }, async t => {
  const cwd = fixture(t);
  await deploy("staging", "snapback", { cwd, args: ["--staging"] });
  let host = await startServe("staging", { cwd, seedUsers });
  try {
    assert.equal((await config(host)).environment, "staging");
    assert.equal((await config(host)).development, false);
    for (const [role, accounts] of Object.entries(host.sessions)) {
      assert.equal(accounts.length, 2);
      for (const account of accounts) {
        assert.ok(account.principal.startsWith("user:"));
        const signed = await login(host.url, account.email, account.password); assert.equal(signed.ok, true);
        const me = await post(host, "/q/myRole", signed.session.token); assert.equal(me.data.role, role);
        assert.equal((await post(host, "/m/add", signed.session.token, { body: "real user data" })).state, "sent");
        const forged = await post(host, "/m/provision", signed.session.token, { principal: account.principal, role: "admin" });
        assert.equal(forged.why.code, "NOT_OPERATOR");
      }
    }
    for (const path of ["/seed", "/dev/reset", "/dev/clock/advance", "/dev/jobs/foo", "/dev/token"]) {
      assert.equal((await fetch(host.url + path, { method: "POST", body: "[]" })).status, 403, path);
    }
    assert.equal((await fetch(host.url + "/q/myRole", { method: "POST", headers: { "x-snapback-persona": "alice" }, body: '{"args":{}}' })).status, 401);
    const v2 = join(cwd, "v2"); mkdirSync(v2); writeFileSync(join(v2, "schema.q"), schema + "\nquery version():\n  return 2\n");
    await deploy("staging", v2, { cwd }); assert.equal((await config(host)).environment, "staging");
    await rollback("staging", 1, { cwd }); assert.equal((await config(host)).environment, "staging");
    const repeated = await cli(["deploy", "staging", "--schema", "snapback", "--staging"], { cwd });
    assert.equal(repeated.code, 0, repeated.stderr);
    const token = await host.operatorToken();
    for (const file of readdirSync(join(cwd, "logs"))) assert.ok(!readFileSync(join(cwd, "logs", file), "utf8").includes(token), "operator token never enters logs");
  } finally { await host.stop(); }
  host = await startServe("staging", { cwd });
  try { assert.equal((await config(host)).environment, "staging"); } finally { await host.stop(); }
});

    assert.equal(minted, false);

    assert.equal((await post(host, "/q/myRole", account.session.token)).data.role, "admin");
  } finally { await host.stop(); }
  await assert.rejects(startServe("production", { cwd, seedUsers }), /E_STAGING:/, "failed setup cleans up its own host");

});

                    if let Err(why) = session_identity::principal_of(&request, shared) {
                        return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":why}));
                    }
                    std::thread::yield_now();
                    continue;
                }
                break evaluated;
                };
                let (store_id, head) = match metadata { Ok(head) => head, Err(why) => { respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({ "denied": why })); return; } };
                match outcome {
                    Ok(outcome) => {
                        #[derive(serde::Serialize)]
                        struct QueryReply<'a> {
                            #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
                            scopes: &'a Option<Json>,
                            #[serde(rename = "searchScopes", skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]

                            #[serde(rename = "searchRows", skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
