//! Held change polls: JSON whitespace heartbeats, followed by one JSON result.
use super::{
    JsonBuffer, Shared, cors, lock, principal_of, query_params, refusal_json, respond_timed,
    trusted_dev_request,
};
use serde_json::{Value as Json, json};
use std::io;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use tiny_http::Request;

const HEARTBEAT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(2);

pub(super) fn serve(request: Request, shared: &Shared, url: &str) {
    let params = query_params(url);
    let since: u64 = params.get("since").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
    let wait: u64 = super::wait_seconds(url);
    let began = Instant::now();
    let deadline = began + Duration::from_secs(wait);
    let paced = began + Duration::from_millis(100);
    let capability = match principal_of(&request, shared) {
        Ok(principal) => principal,
        Err(refusal) => {
            return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }));
        }
    };
    if let Err(denied) = crate::device_handshake::check(shared, crate::device_handshake::header(&request), false) {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 409, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&denied) }));
    }
    let connected = &capability.0;
    // @ref LLP 3000.036#6-idle-live-clients-must-not-cost-a-thread — one principal's tabs cannot take the whole viewer pool; refused at once, never queued.
    let _share = if wait > 0 {
        match shared.long_waits.enter(connected) {
            Ok(share) => Some(share),
            Err(limit) => return super::long_waits::refuse_share(request, shared, limit),
        }
    } else { None };
    let present = capability.2 == snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full;
    let heartbeat = params.get("heartbeat").is_some_and(|s| s == "1") && request.http_version().0 == 1 && request.http_version().1 >= 1;
    let mut response = PollResponse { request: Some(request), started: false, shared };
    let initial = { let wake=lock(&shared.wake); (wake.seq,wake.eph.version.clone(),wake.generation) };
    let epoch = params.get("eph").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(initial.1.epoch);
    let incarnation = params.get("ephi").cloned().unwrap_or(initial.1.incarnation);
    let interest: Option<std::collections::BTreeSet<String>> = params.get("ephTables").map(|s| {
        s.split(',').filter(|s|!s.is_empty()).map(str::to_owned).collect()
    });
    let wants_ephemeral = ["eph", "ephi", "ephTables"].iter().any(|k|params.contains_key(*k));
    {
        let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
        if let Err(why) = super::session_identity::persona_read_authority(response.request.as_ref().unwrap(), shared, &engine) {
            drop(engine); response.finish(401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})); return;
        }
        if present && let Err(error) = engine.presence_reconnected(&connected, shared.now()) {
            response.finish(500, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&error.refusal()) })); return;
        }
        if present { shared.presence.enter(&connected); }
    }
    let result = (|| -> Result<_, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
        // Entry always reads the store: the client may already know a head
        // committed after the last publication (LLP 3000 finding 5).
        let mut current=read_answer(shared, since)?;
        let mut observed=initial.0;
        let mut sequence_wake=false;
        let mut beat=Instant::now();
        loop {
            // The deadline is private scheduling state. Production polls wake
            // at it even without another application request; development
            // clock jumps run the same advancement through their control
            // request and notify this condition variable.
            let opening = if shared.personas {
                None
            } else {
                shared.next_time_audience()?
            };
            // Snapshot and wait share one mutex. Commit cannot pass between
            // the predicate check and the condvar's atomic unlock-and-wait.
            let wake=lock(&shared.wake);
            if wake.seq != observed {
                observed=wake.seq; sequence_wake=true; drop(wake);
                current=answer(shared, since)?;
                continue;
            }
            let tables=wake.eph.changed(epoch,Some(&incarnation),interest.as_ref());
            let reset=wake.eph.version.incarnation!=incarnation;
            let eph_ready=reset||!tables.is_empty();
            let durable=wake.generation != initial.2 || sequence_wake || current.0!=since || params.get("store_id").is_some_and(|id|Some(id)!=current.1.as_ref());
            let now=Instant::now();
            if durable || now>=deadline || eph_ready && now>=paced {
                let eph=snapback4_core::engine::ephemeral::Change {preserved:wake.eph.preserved_since(epoch,&incarnation),epoch:wake.eph.version.epoch,incarnation:wake.eph.version.incarnation.clone(),tables};
                let generation=wake.generation;
                // Replacement publishes its identity with the incarnation. No
                // engine lock is needed to finish an ephemeral-only wake.
                if reset {current.1=wake.store_id.clone();}
                return Ok((current,generation,eph));
            }
            if heartbeat && now>=beat {
                drop(wake); response.heartbeat().map_err(|e|snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
                beat=Instant::now()+HEARTBEAT;continue;
            }
            let mut until=if heartbeat {deadline.min(beat)} else {deadline};
            if eph_ready {until=until.min(paced);}
            if let Some(opening) = opening {
                until = until.min(Instant::now() + shared.time_audience_delay(opening));
            }
            #[cfg(test)]
            super::test_work(&shared.poll_wait);
            let (wake, timeout)=shared.polls.wait_timeout(wake,until.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now())).unwrap_or_else(|e|e.into_inner());
            #[cfg(test)]
            super::test_work(&shared.poll_resumed);
            #[cfg(test)]
            if !timeout.timed_out() { super::test_work(&shared.poll_notified); }
            drop(wake);
            if timeout.timed_out()
                && opening.is_some_and(|at| shared.time_audience_delay(at).is_zero())
            {
                shared.advance_time_audiences()?;
                current = answer(shared, since)?;
            }
        }
    })();
    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.2 — a reader never enters OR leaves presence.
    if present { shared.presence.leave(&connected); }
    // Recheck bearer liveness on a pooled detached reader. Ephemeral-only
    // wakes must not reacquire the serving engine merely to finish a poll.
    let auth_store_id = lock(&shared.wake).store_id.clone();
    let bearer = response.request.as_ref().unwrap().headers().iter().find(|h|h.field.equiv("Authorization")).and_then(|h|crate::auth::parse_bearer(h.value.as_str())).map(|token| token.trim().to_owned());
    let authenticated = match bearer.map(|token| (shared.reader(), token)) {
        None => Ok(()),
        Some((Ok((reader_epoch, reader)), token)) => {
            let resolved = match snapback4_core::identity::resolve_read_only_supervised(reader.store(),&token,snapback4_core::identity::ServiceSample { now: shared.now(), supervisor_revision: shared.worker_revision.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire) },auth_store_id.as_deref()) {
                Ok(Some(current)) if current == capability => Ok(Ok(())),Ok(_)=>Ok(Err(snapback4_core::identity::diagnostics::refused(reader.store(), &token, shared.now(), trusted_dev_request(response.request.as_ref().unwrap(), shared)))),Err(e)=>Err(e),
            };
            match resolved {
                Ok(outcome) => { lock(&shared.readers).put(&reader_epoch, reader.into_inner()); outcome }
                // As for answers: a reader older than a finishing adoption is
                // not a verdict on the session. Ask again behind the engine.
                Err(_) => {
                    principal_of(response.request.as_ref().unwrap(),shared).and_then(|current| {
                        if current == capability { Ok(()) } else { Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "the poll credential changed")) }
                    })
                }
            }
        }
        Some((Err(_), _)) => {
            principal_of(response.request.as_ref().unwrap(),shared).and_then(|current| {
                if current == capability { Ok(()) } else { Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "the poll credential changed")) }
            })
        }
    };
    if let Err(refusal)=authenticated { response.finish(401,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal)}));return; }
    match result {
        Ok(((seq,store_id,tables,preserved,changes),generation,ephemeral))=>{
            let mut reply=json!({"seq":seq,"tables":tables,"preserved":preserved,"changes":changes,"generation":generation,"store_id":store_id});
            if wants_ephemeral {reply["ephemeral"]=json!(ephemeral);}
            response.finish(200,reply);
        }
        Err(error)=>{let why=error.refusal();response.finish(if why.retryable {503}else{500},json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));}
    }
}

type Checked = (u64, Option<String>, Vec<String>, Vec<String>, Json);
// @ref LLP 3000.036#6-idle-live-clients-must-not-cost-a-thread — one commit wakes every held poll; they share one answer per cursor.
/// Wake answers by (cursor, published head, generation, store identity). Only
/// a held poll woken by a publication asks here, and an answer is computed
/// after reading that publication, so its snapshot is newer than the poll's
/// entry and than anything its client knew when it asked. The answer names
/// no viewer, so polls from the same cursor share one read: a commit costs one
/// read per distinct cursor, not one per held poll. Entry and unheld polls
/// read the store themselves (the published counter is a wake signal, never
/// the source of truth). A shared answer is at most a second old and never
/// below the cursor; failures are not kept.
#[derive(Default)]
pub(crate) struct Answers { entries: std::collections::VecDeque<((u64, u64, u64, Option<String>), Instant, Checked)> }
const ANSWERS_KEPT: usize = 64;
const ANSWER_AGE: Duration = Duration::from_secs(1);

fn answer(shared: &Shared, since: u64) -> Result<Checked, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
    let key = { let wake = lock(&shared.wake); (since, wake.seq, wake.generation, wake.store_id.clone()) };
    let mut answers = lock(&shared.change_answers);
    if let Some((_, _, hit)) = answers.entries.iter().find(|(k, at, hit)| *k == key && at.elapsed() < ANSWER_AGE && hit.0 >= since) {
        return Ok(hit.clone());
    }
    let checked = read_answer(shared, since)?;
    // Older publications are never asked for again.
    answers.entries.retain(|(k, at, _)| k.1 >= key.1 && *k != key && at.elapsed() < ANSWER_AGE);
    if answers.entries.len() >= ANSWERS_KEPT { answers.entries.pop_front(); }
    answers.entries.push_back((key, Instant::now(), checked.clone()));
    Ok(checked)
}

fn read_answer(shared: &Shared, since: u64) -> Result<Checked, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
    let _timing = super::latency::EngineSpan::new();
    #[cfg(test)]
    super::test_work(&shared.changes_refresh);
    if let Ok((epoch, mut reader)) = shared.reader() {
        // A pooled reader can predate an adoption that has committed but not
        // yet replaced the pool. Only a success is kept; anything else is read
        // again behind the engine, which waits for that adoption to finish.
        if let Ok(checked) = check(&mut reader, since) {
            lock(&shared.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
            return Ok(checked);
        }
    }
    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
    match shared.open_reader(&engine) {
        Ok(mut reader) => { drop(engine); check(&mut reader, since) }
        // A memory store has no detached reader: read under the engine.
        Err(_) => check(&mut engine, since),
    }
}

/// The notification's head, identity and invalidations share one read snapshot.
fn check(engine: &mut snapback4_core::engine::Engine, since: u64) -> Result<(u64, Option<String>, Vec<String>, Vec<String>, Json), snapback4_core::error::RuntimeError,
> {
    use rusqlite::OptionalExtension;
    let all_tables: Vec<_> = engine.schema().visible_tables().map(|(n,_)|n.clone()).collect();
    let schema = engine.schema().clone();
    let tx = engine.store_mut().read_transaction()?;
    let head = tx.next_seq()? - 1;
    let store_id = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'store_id'", [], |row| row.get(0)).optional()?;
    let changes = tx.commits_after(since, 500)?;
    // Advancing to the actual head must not skip invalidation beyond the
    // bounded commit page. Saturation conservatively invalidates every table.
    let mut tables = if changes.last().is_some_and(|(seq, _)| *seq < head) { all_tables }
        else { changes.into_iter().flat_map(|(_, tables)| tables).filter(|name| all_tables.contains(name)).collect() };
    tables.sort();
    tables.dedup();
    let preserved = snapback4_core::revocation::preserved_between(tx.conn(), &schema, since, head);
    let changes = settlement_changes(tx.conn(), &schema, since, head);
    tx.rollback()?;
    Ok((head, store_id, tables, preserved, changes))
}

struct PollResponse<'a> {
    request: Option<Request>,
    started: bool,
    shared: &'a Shared,
}

impl PollResponse<'_> {
    fn heartbeat(&mut self) -> io::Result<()> {
        let request = self.request.as_mut().unwrap();
        let mut frame = String::new();
        if !self.started {
            frame.push_str("HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\nCache-Control: no-store, no-transform\r\nX-Accel-Buffering: no\r\nTransfer-Encoding: chunked\r\n");
            // Clock time belongs to the final body, after the hold. A buffering
            // transport must not mistake early headers for an end stamp.
            for header in cors(request).into_iter().chain(request.connection_headers()).chain(super::ephemeral_headers(self.shared)).chain(super::latency::header()) { frame.push_str(&format!("{header}\r\n")); }
            frame.push_str("\r\n");
            self.started = true;
        }
        frame.push_str("1\r\n\n\r\n");
        request.write_response_frame(frame.as_bytes())
    }

    fn finish(mut self, status: u16, mut body: Json) {
        if !self.started { return respond_timed(self.request.take().unwrap(), self.shared, status, body); }
        // Stamp after the hold, including any development clock advance.
        let clock = lock(&self.shared.clock);
        body["serverNow"] = json!(clock.now().max(
                self.shared
                    .time_audience_clock
                    .load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire)
            )
        );
        body["clockRevision"] = json!(clock.revision());
        drop(clock);
        // Once heartbeats began the status is 200; the final JSON still
        // carries a typed denial, including a session revoked during the wait.
        let mut refused = status >= 400 || super::latency::Reply::refused(&body);
        let mut bytes = JsonBuffer(Vec::new());
        if serde_json::to_writer(&mut bytes, &body).is_err() {
            refused = true;
            let refusal = snapback4_core::error::Refusal::new("E_BOUND", "bound", "the HTTP JSON response exceeds 16 MiB; request fewer changes").guide("reads");
            bytes.0.clear();
            serde_json::to_writer(&mut bytes, &json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal), "serverNow": body["serverNow"], "clockRevision": body["clockRevision"] })).unwrap();
        }
        let mut frame = format!("{:x}\r\n", bytes.0.len()).into_bytes();
        frame.extend_from_slice(&bytes.0);
        frame.extend_from_slice(b"\r\n0\r\n\r\n");
        if self.request.as_mut().unwrap().write_response_frame(&frame).is_ok() {
            // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.7 — the final frame completes a held poll, not its first heartbeat.
            super::latency::finished(refused);
            self.request.take().unwrap().finish_stream();
        } else {
            super::latency::finished(true);
        }
    }
}

impl Drop for PollResponse<'_> {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        // A failed or panicking stream cannot append a second response after
        // incomplete chunk framing, including on pipelined connections.
        if let Some(request) = self.request.take() { request.abort(); }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod column_tests {
    #[test]
    fn settlement_intervals_project_private_counts_and_keep_public_fields() {
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), include_str!("../../snapback4-lang/tests/fixtures/maintained-columns.q").into(),
        )]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty());
        let mut engine = snapback4_core::Engine::memory(bound.schema, []).unwrap();
        let maintain = engine.schema().maintains.iter().find(|m| m.private.as_ref().is_some_and(|o| o.column == "likeCount")).unwrap();
        let private = maintain.target.clone();
        let after = engine.seq().unwrap();
        let tx = engine.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();
        tx.record_commit(after + 1, 1000, "like", "alice", "mutation", &["posts".into()], 1).unwrap();
        let proof = serde_json::json!({"unknown":false,"tables":{
            private: {"deleted":false,"population":true,"columns":[]},
            "posts": {"deleted":false,"population":false,"columns":["at"]}
        }});
        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO _change_evidence(seq, footprint) VALUES (?1, ?2)", rusqlite::params![(after + 1) as i64, proof.to_string()]).unwrap();
        tx.commit().unwrap();
        let changes = super::settlement_changes(engine.store().conn(), engine.schema(), after, after + 1);
        assert_eq!(changes, serde_json::json!({"after":after,"through":after+1,"writes":{"posts":["at","likeCount"]}}));
        assert!(!changes.to_string().contains("$column"));
        assert!(super::settlement_changes(engine.store().conn(), engine.schema(), after, after + 2).is_null(), "a gap never certifies fields");
    }
    #[test]
    fn saturated_changes_uses_the_public_table_enumeration() {
        let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),include_str!("../../snapback4-lang/tests/fixtures/maintained-columns.q").into(),
        )]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",bound.diagnostics);
        let mut engine=snapback4_core::Engine::memory(bound.schema,[]).unwrap();
        let private = engine.schema().maintains[0].target.clone();
        assert!(!engine.schema().visible(&private));
        let from = engine.seq().unwrap(); // Initial column activation has its own parent barrier.
        assert_eq!(super::check(&mut engine, 0).unwrap().2, ["posts"]);
        let touched: String = engine.store().conn().query_row("SELECT touched FROM _commits WHERE seq=?1", [from as i64], |r| r.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(serde_json::from_str::<Vec<String>>(&touched).unwrap(), ["posts"]);
        let events: i64 = engine.store().conn().query_row("SELECT count(*) FROM _log WHERE seq=?1", [from as i64], |r| r.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(events, 0);
        let tx = engine.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();
        tx.record_commit(from + 1, 1000, "legacy", "alice", "mutation", &[private, "likes".into()], 1).unwrap();
        tx.commit().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(super::check(&mut engine, from).unwrap().2, ["likes"]);
        let tx=engine.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();
        for seq in from + 2..=from + 501 {tx.record_commit(seq,1000,"like","alice","mutation",&["likes".into()],1).unwrap()}
        tx.commit().unwrap();
        let (seq,_,tables,_,_)=super::check(&mut engine,from).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(seq,from + 501);
        assert_eq!(tables,["likes","posts"]);
    }
}

pub(super) fn ack_preserved(engine: &snapback4_core::Engine, outcome: Option<&snapback4_core::MutationOutcome>) -> Vec<String> {
    match outcome {
        Some(out) if !out.replayed => match out.state {
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent { .. } if out.receipt_only => {
                snapback4_core::revocation::Footprint::default().preserved(engine.schema())
            }
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent { seq, .. } => {
                snapback4_core::revocation::preserved_between(engine.store().conn(), engine.schema(), seq.saturating_sub(1), seq)
            }
            _ => Vec::new(),
        }, _ => Vec::new(),
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.023#11 — certify the whole interval, including server-only inputs.
pub(super) fn settlement_changes(conn: &rusqlite::Connection, schema: &snapback4_core::Schema, after: u64, through: u64) -> Json {
    let Some(f) = snapback4_core::revocation::footprint_between(conn, after, through).filter(|f| !f.unknown) else { return Json::Null; };
    let mut public = snapback4_core::revocation::Footprint::default();
    for (name, c) in f.tables {
        if schema.visible(&name) {
            let target = public.tables.entry(name).or_default();
            target.deleted |= c.deleted; target.population |= c.population; target.columns.extend(c.columns);
        }
        else if let Some(owner) = schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m| m.target == name).find_map(|m| m.private.as_ref()) {
            // Private aggregate state changes its public owner field, never leaks its formula/table.
            public.tables.entry(owner.table.clone()).or_default().columns.insert(owner.column.clone());
        } else { return Json::Null; }
    }
    let writes: serde_json::Map<String, Json> = public.tables.into_iter().map(|(name, c)| {
            (name, if c.population || c.deleted { Json::Null } else { json!(c.columns) },
            )
        }).collect();
    json!({"after":after,"through":through,"writes":writes})
}

//! Immutable source releases with a database-authoritative activation pointer.
use super::*;
use crate::store_owner::Owner;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
#[path = "staging.rs"]
mod staging;
struct Stage(PathBuf);
impl Drop for Stage {fn drop(&mut self){let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}

fn directory(options: &Options) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    options
        .positionals
        .first()
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .ok_or_else(|| "a deployment directory is required".into())
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5.1 — deployment aliases share ownership, control and worker paths.
fn canonical_directory(options: &Options) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    let requested = directory(options)?;
    if !requested.exists() {
        snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&requested).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    let dir = requested.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    Ok(dir)
}

/// `dev` and `serve` hold the store-owner lock for their lifetime. Release
/// commands route to the admitted server when it owns this deployment.
pub(crate) fn deployment_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> {
    crate::store_owner::command(&dir.join("store.sqlite"))
}
fn current(dir: &Path, owner: &Owner) -> Result<Option<u64>, String> {
    let database = dir.join("store.sqlite");
    if database.exists() {
        let store = owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if let Some(release) = store.meta("release").map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
            return release
                .parse()
                .map(Some)
                .map_err(|_| "invalid release identity in store".into());
        }
    }
    // Compatibility with deployments made before the database owned this
    // pointer. The next activation records it transactionally.
    match std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("current")) {
        Ok(text) => text
            .trim()
            .parse()
            .map(Some)
            .map_err(|_| "invalid current release file".into()),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
        Err(e) => Err(format!("cannot read current release: {e}")),
    }
}
pub(super) fn bound_at(root: PathBuf) -> Result<snapback4_lang::Bound, String> {
    let project = Project { root };
    if project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_empty() {
        return Err("release has no Quarry source; select a complete immutable release".into());
    }
    let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        return Err(bound
            .diagnostics
            .iter()
            .map(ToString::to_string)
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join("\n"));
    }
    Ok(bound)
}
fn check_change(
    dir: &Path,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound,
    options: &Options,
    target_release: Option<u64>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    if !options.flags.contains_key("check") && let Some(n) = previous {
        let old = bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string()))?;
        let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let target_label = target_release.map(|n| format!("release {n}")).unwrap_or_else(|| "the new release".into());
        let lines = super::release_narration::compatibility(&store, &old.schema, &bound.schema, n, &target_label, target_release.is_none())?;
        for line in lines {
            eprintln!("{line}");
        }
    }
    if options.flags.contains_key("allow-destructive") {
        eprintln!(
            "deploy/rollback retain storage; --allow-destructive does not bypass validation or prune. To drop dormant storage: snapback4 prune {} --allow-destructive",
            dir.display()
        );
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn command_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<std::fs::File, String> {
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(
            &dir.join("store.sqlite"),
            &dir.join(".release-command"),
            true,
            0,
        )
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        file.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

//! Request-side generation discovery; row-page wire forms remain in serve.rs.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::error::RuntimeError;

enum SyncOutcome<T> {
    Generation(Json),
    Version(Json),
    Page(T),
}

#[cfg(test)]
pub(super) static MATERIALIZATIONS: Mutex<BTreeMap<String, usize>> = Mutex::new(BTreeMap::new());

// @ref LLP 3000.007.003#5 — learn the answering generation before materializing a stale page.
fn under_generation<T>(engine: &mut Engine, requested: Option<u64>, generation: u64, store_id: &str, device: &snapback4_core::device_protocol::DeviceEngine,
    materialize: impl FnOnce(&mut Engine) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>,
) -> Result<SyncOutcome<T>, RuntimeError> {
    if let Err(denied) = snapback4_core::device_protocol::check(Some(device), &snapback4_core::device_protocol::compatibility(&engine.schema().public_projection())) {
        return Ok(SyncOutcome::Version(json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&denied) })));
    }
    if requested.is_some_and(|requested| requested != generation) {
        let denied = Refusal::new("E_SYNC_GENERATION", "generation", "sync generation changed; adopt /schema before retrying this position").guide("sync");
        // Same head as a caught-up page: MAX(_commits.seq), not a delivered cut.
        let watermark = engine.seq()?;
        let reply = json!({
            "denied": refusal_json(&denied), "generation": generation,
            "store_id": store_id, "watermark": watermark,
        });
        return Ok(SyncOutcome::Generation(reply));
    }
    let page = materialize(engine)?;
    #[cfg(test)]
    { *lock(&MATERIALIZATIONS).entry(store_id.to_owned()).or_default() += 1; }
    Ok(SyncOutcome::Page(page))
}

pub(super) fn serve(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str, now: i64) {
    // This is also the page path's request authorization. Partition rules
    // filter unreadable rows/groups; they add no whole-request refusal.
    let principal = match principal_of(&request, shared) {
        Ok(principal) => principal.0,
        Err(refusal) => {
            respond_timed(request, shared, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }));
            return;
        }
    };
    // The header envelope is parsed before SyncRequest, so an old wire shape
    // cannot fall through to a missing-field or schema-decoder refusal.
    if let Err(denied) = crate::device_handshake::check(shared, crate::device_handshake::header(&request), true) {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 409, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&denied) }));
    }
    let device: snapback4_core::device_protocol::DeviceEngine = serde_json::from_str(crate::device_handshake::header(&request).expect("checked handshake")).expect("checked handshake");
    let input = match serde_json::from_str::<SyncRequest>(body) {
        Ok(input) if input.pending.len() <= 256 && input.pending.iter().all(|id| !id.is_empty() && id.len() <= 128) =>
        {
            input
        }
        _ => {
            return respond_timed(request, shared, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "sync needs { generation?, from, limit, after, stream, pending }: nonnegative integers, a cursor or null, a boolean, and at most 256 ids of 1..128 UTF-8 bytes")) }));
        }
    };
    // @ref LLP 3000.026#4-atomic-launch-adoption — sync has its own bounded
    // admission pool and authenticates before waiting on atomic launch.
    let launching = lock(&shared.readers).launching_feed();
    if launching {
        let engine = lock(&shared.engine);
        // A long wait cannot preserve an expired or revoked bearer.
        let authority = mutation_authority(&request, shared, &engine, &principal);
        drop(engine);
        if let Err(refusal) = authority {
            respond_timed(request, shared, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }));
            return;
        }
    }
    let SyncRequest { generation: requested_generation, from, limit, mut after, stream, pending, store_id: requested_store } = input;
    let store_id = match shared.read_head() {
        Ok((id, _)) => id,
        Err(error) => {
            return respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&error) }));
        }
    };
    // An old snapshot cursor belongs to its database, not merely its schema.
    if requested_store.as_ref().is_some_and(|id| id != &store_id) { after = None; }
    let limit = limit.clamp(1, 10_000);
    if from == 0 && !stream {
        // `after` is the previous snapshot page's opaque `next` token.
        let (outcome, generation) = {
            let reader = shared.reader().map(|(_,reader)|reader);
            match reader {
                Ok(mut reader) => {
                    if let Err(why) = super::session_identity::persona_read_authority(&request, shared, &reader) {
                        return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));
                    }
                    let generation = reader.generation();
                    (under_generation(&mut reader, requested_generation, generation, &store_id, &device, |reader| {
                                reader.snapshot_acquisition_page_at(&principal, after.as_deref(), limit, now).and_then(|page| {
                                        reader.acknowledged(&principal, &pending, page.watermark).map(|acks| (page, acks))
                                    })
                            },
                        ), generation,
                    )
                }
                Err(error) if !shared.memory_store() => {
                    return respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({"denied": error.refusal()}));
                }
                Err(_) => {
                    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
                    if let Err(why) = super::session_identity::persona_read_authority(&request, shared, &engine) {
                        drop(engine); return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));
                    }
                    let generation = engine.generation();
                    (under_generation(&mut engine, requested_generation, generation, &store_id, &device, |engine| {
                                engine.snapshot_acquisition_page_at(&principal, after.as_deref(), limit, now).and_then(|page| {
                                        engine.acknowledged(&principal, &pending, page.watermark).map(|acks| (page, acks))
                                    })
                            },
                        ), generation,
                    )
                }
            }
        };
        if shared.memory_store() {
            shared.publish_engine(&lock(&shared.engine));
        }
        match outcome {
            Ok(SyncOutcome::Page((page, acknowledged))) => {
                let next = page.next.map(|next| next.encode());
                let (events, watermark, held) = (page.events, page.watermark, page.held);
                let overflow = json!({ "generation": generation, "store_id": store_id, "watermark": watermark });
                respond_with_sync(request, 200, SnapshotReply {
                    sent: acknowledged.clone(),
                    acquisitions: page.acquisitions, acknowledged, events: SyncEvents::Snapshot(events), generation, store_id,
                    held, more: next.is_some(), next, after, now, snapshot: true, watermark,
                }, true, Some(overflow), Some(shared));
            }
            Ok(SyncOutcome::Version(reply)) => respond_timed(request, shared, 409, reply),
            Ok(SyncOutcome::Generation(reply)) => respond_timed(request, shared, 200, reply),
            Err(error) => { let refusal = error.refusal(); respond_timed(request, shared, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else if refusal.code == "E_INPUT" { 400 } else { 500 }, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) })) }
        }
    } else {
        let (outcome, generation) = {
            let reader = shared.reader().map(|(_,reader)|reader);
            match reader {
                Ok(mut reader) => {
                    if let Err(why) = super::session_identity::persona_read_authority(&request, shared, &reader) {
                        return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));
                    }
                    let generation = reader.generation();
                    (under_generation(&mut reader, requested_generation, generation, &store_id, &device, |reader| {
                                reader.stream_at(&principal, from, limit, now).and_then(|page| {
                                        reader.acknowledged_at(&principal, &pending, page.watermark, page.images_at.unwrap_or(page.watermark)).map(|acks| { let preserved = snapback4_core::revocation::preserved_between(reader.store().conn(), reader.schema(), from, page.images_at.unwrap_or(page.watermark)); let changes = super::changes::settlement_changes(reader.store().conn(), reader.schema(), from, page.images_at.unwrap_or(page.watermark)); (page, acks, preserved, changes) })
                                    })
                            },
                        ), generation,
                    )
                }
                Err(error) if !shared.memory_store() => {
                    return respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({"denied": error.refusal()}));
                }
                Err(_) => {
                    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
                    if let Err(why) = super::session_identity::persona_read_authority(&request, shared, &engine) {
                        drop(engine); return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));
                    }
                    let generation = engine.generation();
                    (under_generation(&mut engine, requested_generation, generation, &store_id, &device, |engine| {
                                engine.stream_at(&principal, from, limit, now).and_then(|page| {
                                        engine.acknowledged_at(&principal, &pending, page.watermark, page.images_at.unwrap_or(page.watermark)).map(|acks| { let preserved = snapback4_core::revocation::preserved_between(engine.store().conn(), engine.schema(), from, page.images_at.unwrap_or(page.watermark)); let changes = super::changes::settlement_changes(engine.store().conn(), engine.schema(), from, page.images_at.unwrap_or(page.watermark)); (page, acks, preserved, changes) })
                                    })
                            },
                        ), generation,
                    )
                }
            }
        };
        if shared.memory_store() {
            shared.publish_engine(&lock(&shared.engine));
        }
        match outcome {
            Ok(SyncOutcome::Page((page, (acknowledged, committed_above), preserved, changes))) => {
                let mut overflow = json!({ "generation": generation, "store_id": store_id, "watermark": page.watermark });
                // Whole commits cannot be split by lowering the stream limit.
                // A snapshot pages their current rows individually instead.
                if limit == 1 { overflow["snapshot"] = json!(true); }
                respond_with_sync(request, 200, StreamReply {
                    preserved, changes,
                    sent: acknowledged.iter().chain(committed_above.iter()).map(|(id, seq)| (id.clone(), *seq)).collect(),
                    images_at: page.images_at, committed_above, acquisitions: page.acquisitions, acknowledged, events: SyncEvents::Stream(page.events), generation, store_id,
                    joined: page.joined, more: page.more, now, snapshot: false, watermark: page.watermark,
                }, true, Some(overflow), Some(shared));
            }
            Ok(SyncOutcome::Version(reply)) => respond_timed(request, shared, 409, reply),
            Ok(SyncOutcome::Generation(reply)) => respond_timed(request, shared, 200, reply),
            Err(RuntimeError::Bound { .. }) => {
                let refusal = Refusal::new("E_BOUND", "bound", "the HTTP JSON response exceeds 16 MiB; request a smaller page or return fewer values").guide("reads");
                let mut reply = json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal), "generation": generation,
                    "store_id": store_id, "watermark": from });
                if limit == 1 { reply["snapshot"] = json!(true); }
                respond_timed(request, shared, 200, reply)
            }
            Err(error) => { let refusal = error.refusal(); respond_timed(request, shared, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 500 }, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) })) }
        }
    }
}

#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct SyncRequest {
    #[serde(default)]
    generation: Option<u64>,
    #[serde(default)]
    from: u64,
    #[serde(default = "sync_limit")]
    limit: usize,
    #[serde(default)]
    after: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    stream: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pending: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    store_id: Option<String>,
}
fn sync_limit() -> usize { 2_000 }

//! Authenticated, bounded controller transport. Custody is per consumer, never
//! an operation-global cursor shared by tabs. Bodies contain no work counters.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::engine::following_history::{Request as UnitRequest, projection};

pub(super) fn serve(request: Request, shared: &Shared, operation: &str, body: &str) {
    if body.len() > 64 * 1024 {
        return respond_timed(
            request,
            shared,
            400,
            json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}),
        );
    }
    let (principal, _, scope) = match principal_of(&request, shared) {
        Ok(p) => p,
        Err(r) => return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&r)})),
    };
    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.2 — Following custody is outside the closed read allow-list.
    // The outer guard refuses before body intake; retain the boundary here too.
    if scope == snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Read {
        let why = Refusal::new(
            "E_READ_ONLY",
            "auth",
            "Following history requires a full session; ordinary inbox queries remain available",
        )
        .guide("read-only");
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));
    }
    let payload: Json = match serde_json::from_str(body) {
        Ok(v) => v,
        Err(_) => {
            return respond_timed(
                request,
                shared,
                400,
                json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}),
            );
        }
    };
    // Lock order is engine then controller. Authentication is checked again at
    // the same engine incarnation that owns the source read transaction.
    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
    crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());
    if let Err(r) = mutation_authority(&request, shared, &engine, &principal) {
        drop(engine);
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&r)}));
    }
    let Some(program) = engine.program(operation) else {
        drop(engine);
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({"phase":"restart"}));
    };
    let Some(plan) = projection::plan(engine.schema(), program) else {
        drop(engine);
        return respond_timed(
            request,
            shared,
            200,
            json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_DECLARATION"}),
        );
    };
    let binding = plan.binding;
    let mut args = BTreeMap::new();
    let Some(fields) = payload.get("args").and_then(Json::as_object) else {
        drop(engine);
        return respond_timed(
            request,
            shared,
            400,
            json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}),
        );
    };
    if let Some(why)=super::dev_doors::persona_argument(shared,&program.args,&Json::Object(fields.clone())) {
        drop(engine);return respond_timed(request,shared,400,json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT","message":why.message,"denied":why}));
    }
    for (name, value) in fields {
        let Some(value) = program
            .args
            .get(name)
            .and_then(|kind| wire::from_json(kind, value).ok())
        else {
            drop(engine);
            return respond_timed(
                request,
                shared,
                400,
                json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}),
            );
        };
        args.insert(name.clone(), value);
    }
    let now = shared.now();
    let mut reader = lock(&shared.following);
    let action = payload
        .get("action")
        .and_then(Json::as_str)
        .unwrap_or("unit");
    let answer = if action == "open" {
        reader
            .opening_projected(&engine, operation, Context::new(&principal, args).at(now))
            .map(|o| json!({"opening":o.request.opening}).to_string())
    } else if let Some(opening) = payload.get("opening").and_then(Json::as_str) {
        if action == "cancel" {
            reader
                .cancel_projected(
                    &mut engine,
                    opening,
                    operation,
                    Context::new(&principal, args).at(now),
                )
                .map(|_| json!({"phase":"restart"}).to_string())
        } else if action == "refresh" {
            let mounted = serde_json::from_value::<
                Vec<snapback4_core::engine::following_history::Mounted>,
            >(payload.get("mounted").cloned().unwrap_or(json!([])));
            let seen = serde_json::from_value::<Vec<String>>(
                payload.get("seen").cloned().unwrap_or(json!([])),
            );
            match (mounted, seen) {
                (Ok(mounted), Ok(seen)) => reader
                    .refresh_slice_metered(
                        &mut engine,
                        opening,
                        operation,
                        Context::new(&principal, args).at(now),
                        &mounted,
                        &seen,
                    )
                    .map(|(r, _)| serde_json::to_string(&r).unwrap()),
                _ => Ok(json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}).to_string()),
            }
        } else if action == "unit"
            && payload
                .get("token")
                .is_some_and(|v| !v.is_null() && !v.is_string())
        {
            Ok(json!({"phase":"restart"}).to_string())
        } else if action == "unit" {
            let q = UnitRequest {
                declaration: binding.declaration,
                page_size: binding.page_size,
                opening: opening.into(),
                token: payload
                    .get("token")
                    .and_then(Json::as_str)
                    .map(str::to_owned),
            };
            reader
                .request_projected(
                    &mut engine,
                    q,
                    Context::new(&principal, args).at(now),
                    operation,
                )
                .map(|out| out.answer.bytes().to_owned())
        } else {
            Ok(json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}).to_string())
        }
    } else {
        Ok(json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}).to_string())
    };
    drop(reader);
    drop(engine);
    let bytes =
        answer.unwrap_or_else(|e| json!({"phase":"refused","code":e.refusal().code}).to_string());
    // Do not parse/re-encode a pinned response or attach a fresh store counter.
    let mut response = Response::from_string(bytes);
    if let Some(header)=crate::serve::generation_header(){response.add_header(header);}
    response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("Content-Type", "application/json").unwrap());
    response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("Cache-Control", "private, no-store").unwrap());
    for header in cors(&request).into_iter().chain(latency::header()) {
        response.add_header(header);
    }
    let _ = request.respond(response);
}

/bin/bash -lc 'timeout 10s cat snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/store_owner.rs' in /home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b16-stage
 succeeded in 0ms:
//! Ownership locks the database's sidecar; stat pins its inode. `serve.lock` is only an address hint.
use std::{fs::{self, File}, io::{Read, Seek, SeekFrom, Write}, path::{Path, PathBuf}, time::Duration};
use snapback4_core::{Engine, RuntimeError, Store};
#[cfg(unix)]
use std::os::unix::fs::MetadataExt;

pub(crate) struct Owner {
    _sidecar: std::sync::Arc<File>,
    identity: (u64, u64),
    path: PathBuf,
    record: Option<File>,
    reclaimed: Option<String>,
    pub(crate) token_authority: Option<crate::serve::token::Authority>,
}

/// The address `doctor` dials for the server that owns this store: the
/// published loopback address itself, or, for a wildcard bind, that family's
/// loopback on the same port. A published non-loopback address is not followed.
pub(crate) fn inspector_address(store: &Path) -> Option<std::net::SocketAddr> {
    let path = store.canonicalize().ok()?;
    dialable(&address(&path)?)
}

fn dialable(published: &str) -> Option<std::net::SocketAddr> {
    use std::net::{IpAddr, Ipv4Addr, Ipv6Addr, SocketAddr};
    let address = published.strip_prefix("http://")?.parse::<SocketAddr>().ok()?;
    let ip = match address.ip() {
        ip if ip.is_loopback() => ip,
        IpAddr::V4(ip) if ip.is_unspecified() => IpAddr::V4(Ipv4Addr::LOCALHOST),
        IpAddr::V6(ip) if ip.is_unspecified() => IpAddr::V6(Ipv6Addr::LOCALHOST),
        _ => return None,
    };
    (address.port() != 0).then(|| SocketAddr::new(ip, address.port()))
}

fn resolve(store: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    match store.canonicalize() {
        Ok(path) => Ok(path),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
            let parent = store.parent().filter(|p| !p.as_os_str().is_empty()).unwrap_or(Path::new("."));
            snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(parent).map_err(|e| failure(store, e))?;
            Ok(parent.canonicalize().map_err(|e| failure(store, e))?.join(store.file_name().ok_or("store path has no filename")?))
        }
        Err(e) => Err(failure(store, e)),
    }
}

fn failure(path: &Path, reason: impl std::fmt::Display) -> String {
    format!("E_STORE at {}: {reason}; restore a regular store/address file and retry", path.display())
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn same_file(a: &fs::Metadata, b: &fs::Metadata) -> bool { a.dev() == b.dev() && a.ino() == b.ino() }

#[cfg(unix)]
fn regular(path: &Path, create: bool, database: &Path) -> Result<File, String> {
    snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(database, path, create, 128)
        .map_err(|e| failure(path, e))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — sidecar flocks fence backend writers and share device ownership without locking SQLite.
pub(crate) fn command(store: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> { acquire(store, false, Duration::ZERO) }
pub(crate) fn device(store: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> { acquire(store, true, Duration::ZERO) }
// @ref LLP 3000.009#5.3 — a restarting host waits for watchdogs to finish group cleanup.
pub(crate) fn host(store: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> { acquire(store, false, Duration::from_secs(5)) }

#[cfg(unix)]
fn acquire(store: &Path, shared: bool, wait: Duration) -> Result<Owner, String> {
    let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + wait;
    for _ in 0..16 {
        let path = resolve(store)?;
        snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(path.parent().ok_or("store path has no parent")?)
            .map_err(|e| failure(&path, e))?;
        // Stat before opening either auxiliary file; a refused probe creates
        // no database. Only the sidecar lock grants permission to create one.
        let before = snapback4_core::private_fs::database_metadata(&path).map_err(|e| failure(&path, e))?;
        let sidecar = snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_file(&path)
            .map_err(|e| failure(&snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_path(&path), e))?;
        let mut cleanup = None;
        loop {
            let locked = if shared { sidecar.try_lock_shared() } else { sidecar.try_lock() };
            match locked {
                Ok(()) => break,
                Err(fs::TryLockError::WouldBlock) if std::time::Instant::now() < deadline && *cleanup.get_or_insert_with(|| stopped_host(&path)) => std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(25)),
                Err(fs::TryLockError::WouldBlock) => return Err(busy(&path)),
                Err(fs::TryLockError::Error(error)) => return Err(failure(&path, error)),
            }
        }
        if !snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_is_named(&path, &sidecar) { continue; }
        snapback4_core::private_fs::database_file(&path).map_err(|e| failure(&path, e))?;
        let database = snapback4_core::private_fs::database_metadata(&path).map_err(|e| failure(&path, e))?
            .ok_or_else(|| failure(&path, "database disappeared during ownership acquisition"))?;
        if before.as_ref().is_some_and(|old| !same_file(old, &database)) { continue; }
        let mut reclaimed = None;
        let record = if shared { None } else {
            let record_path = path.with_file_name("serve.lock");
            if let Some(pid)=owner_pid(&path) {
                if unsafe {libc::kill(pid as libc::pid_t,0)} == -1 && std::io::Error::last_os_error().raw_os_error()==Some(libc::ESRCH) {
                    reclaimed = Some(format!("reclaimed stale serve.lock: dead PID {pid}; directory {}", path.parent().unwrap().display()));
                }
            }
            let file = regular(&record_path, true, &path)?;
            file.set_len(0).map_err(|e| failure(&record_path, e))?;
            Some(file)
        };
        return Ok(Owner { _sidecar: std::sync::Arc::new(sidecar), identity: (database.dev(), database.ino()), path, record, reclaimed, token_authority: None });
    }
    Err(failure(store, "database path kept changing during ownership acquisition"))
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn acquire(store: &Path, _: bool, _: Duration) -> Result<Owner, String> { Err(failure(store, "database ownership requires Unix")) }

fn stopped_host(path: &Path) -> bool {
    // A CLI owner or a host not yet announced still refuses immediately. A
    // reachable listener does too; only a prior host's cleanup gets a wait.
    // This is a refusal/wait hint, never permission to open the store: the
    // sidecar must still be acquired. Never dial a non-loopback address hint.
    address(path).is_some_and(|published| dialable(&published).is_none_or(|address|
        std::net::TcpStream::connect_timeout(&address, Duration::from_millis(50)).is_err()))
}

pub(crate) fn busy(path: &Path) -> String {
    let owner = address(path).map(|address| match owner_pid(path) { Some(pid) => format!("{address} (pid {pid})"), None => address })
        .unwrap_or_else(|| "another CLI command or a server starting up (address pending)".into());
    format!("E_STORE_BUSY at {}: this store is live; owned by {owner}; directory {}; no work was admitted. Stop its `snapback4 dev` or `snapback4 serve` process (or wait for the owning CLI command), rerun this command once, then restart the server. Exclusion holds while <database>-owner is left in place; deleting or replacing it while a process runs ends exclusion and does not stop that process", path.display(),path.parent().unwrap_or(Path::new(".")).display())
}

#[cfg(unix)]
pub(crate) fn address(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
    // A missing/replaced address record cannot grant ownership. Never follow
    // a symlink (including one to the database), nor block on a FIFO.
    let record = path.with_file_name("serve.lock");
    let file = regular(&record, false, path).ok()?;
    let mut text = String::new();
    file.take(1024).read_to_string(&mut text).ok()?;
    let mut lines = text.lines();
    let port = lines.next().and_then(|s| s.parse::<u16>().ok());
    lines.next().map(str::to_owned).or_else(|| port.map(|p| format!("http://127.0.0.1:{p}")))
}

/// The owning host's PID from its address record (`pid N` on the third line).
#[cfg(unix)]
fn owner_pid(path: &Path) -> Option<u32> {
    let record = path.with_file_name("serve.lock");
    let file = regular(&record, false, path).ok()?;
    let mut text = String::new();
    file.take(1024).read_to_string(&mut text).ok()?;
    text.lines().nth(2)?.strip_prefix("pid ")?.parse().ok()
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn owner_pid(_: &Path) -> Option<u32> { None }

#[cfg(not(unix))]
pub(crate) fn address(_: &Path) -> Option<String> { None }

impl Owner {
    pub(crate) fn report_reclaim(&self, verbose: bool) {
        if verbose && let Some(note) = &self.reclaimed { eprintln!("note: {note}"); }
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — unlink only our address inode; the ownership sidecar remains named.
    pub(crate) fn clear_address(&self) {
        #[cfg(unix)]
        if let Some(file) = &self.record {
            let path = self.path.with_file_name("serve.lock");
            if let (Ok(held), Ok(named)) = (file.metadata(), fs::symlink_metadata(&path))
                && same_file(&held, &named) {
                let _ = fs::remove_file(path);
            }
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn worker_lock(&self) -> std::sync::Arc<File> { self._sidecar.clone() }

    pub(crate) fn path(&self) -> Result<&Path, RuntimeError> {
        #[cfg(unix)]
        {
            let meta = snapback4_core::private_fs::database_metadata(&self.path)
                .map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(failure(&self.path, e)))?;
            if meta.is_some_and(|m| (m.dev(), m.ino()) == self.identity) { return Ok(&self.path); }
        }
        Err(RuntimeError::Store(failure(&self.path, "owned database moved or was replaced; stop and reopen its current path")))
    }

    pub(crate) fn open_store(&self, timeout: Duration) -> Result<Store, RuntimeError> {
        let mut store = Store::open_with_busy_timeout(self.path()?, timeout)?;
        #[cfg(unix)]
        store.retain_ownership(self._sidecar.clone())?;
        Ok(store)
    }

    pub(crate) fn open_engine(&self, bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound, timeout: Duration, now: i64) -> Result<Engine, RuntimeError> {
        let store = self.open_store(timeout)?;
        if store.meta("release")?.is_some() {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("this store belongs to a deployment; use `deploy` to activate source and `serve` to run it".into()));
        }
        Engine::new_at(store, bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now)
    }
}

impl Drop for Owner { fn drop(&mut self) { self.clear_address(); } }

pub(crate) fn announce(owner: &mut Owner, address: std::net::SocketAddr) -> Result<(), String> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — live token work runs through the owner, with proof of local store access.
    owner.token_authority = Some(crate::serve::token::publish(owner.path().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?)
        .map_err(|e| failure(&owner.path, e))?);
    let file = owner.record.as_mut().ok_or("device ownership has no server address record")?;
    file.set_len(0).and_then(|_| file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(0))).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    // First-line port is retained for the dev inspector; the third line names the owning process.
    write!(file, "{}\nhttp://{address}\npid {}\n", address.port(), std::process::id()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
}

export async function proveStaging(client, platform, directory) {
  const cwd = join(resolve(directory), "staging-recipe"); mkdirSync(cwd, { recursive: true });
  const env = { ...process.env, npm_config_audit: "false", npm_config_fund: "false" };
  delete env.SNAPBACK4_BIN; delete env.PORT_LO; delete env.PORT_HI;
  const run = (cmd, args, extra = {}) => runToolchainCommand(cmd, args, { cwd, env: { ...env, ...extra }, timeout: 300_000 });
  writeFileSync(join(cwd, "package.json"), '{"private":true,"type":"module"}');
  await run("npm", ["install", "--ignore-scripts", "--no-audit", "--no-fund", resolve(client), resolve(platform)]);
  const cli = join(cwd, "node_modules/snapback4/bin/snapback4.js");
  const guide = spawnSync(process.execPath, [cli, "guide", "staging", "--full"], { cwd, env, encoding: "utf8", timeout: 30000 });
  // The source of every executed recipe is the
  // installed binary's card, never this checkout's authoring text.
  assert.equal(guide.status, 0, guide.stderr);
  const text = guide.stdout;
  assert.equal(typeof text, "string");
  const schema = [...text.matchAll(/```quarry\n([\s\S]*?)\n```/g)].map(match => match[1]);
  const scripts = [...text.matchAll(/```js\n([\s\S]*?)\n```/g)].map(match => match[1]);
  assert.equal(schema.length, 1); assert.equal(scripts.length, 2);
  mkdirSync(join(cwd, "snapback")); writeFileSync(join(cwd, "snapback/schema.q"), schema[0]);
  await run(process.execPath, [cli, "check"]);
  writeFileSync(join(cwd, "staging.mjs"), scripts[0]); writeFileSync(join(cwd, "first-admin.mjs"), scripts[1]);
  await run(process.execPath, ["staging.mjs", "--once"]);
  await run(process.execPath, [cli, "deploy", "production"]);
  await run(process.execPath, ["first-admin.mjs"], { FIRST_ADMIN_EMAIL: "chosen-admin@example.test", FIRST_ADMIN_PASSWORD: "explicit-production-password" });
  writeFileSync(join(cwd, "verify.mjs"), verify);
  await run(process.execPath, ["verify.mjs"]);
  return { status: "passed", guide: "staging", recipes: "verbatim installed card", roles: { admin: 2, member: 2 },
    mode: "serve", realPasswordLogin: "passed", data: "passed", selfGrant: "refused", productionBulkSeed: "refused before mint", productionRelabel: "refused", firstAdmin: "passed", privateCredentials: "0600" };
}

fn local_authority(authority: &str) -> bool {
    authority_host(authority).is_some_and(|host| host.eq_ignore_ascii_case("localhost") || host.parse::<IpAddr>().is_ok_and(|ip| ip.is_loopback()))
}

fn origin_authority(origin: &str) -> Option<&str> {
    let authority = origin.strip_prefix("http://").or_else(|| origin.strip_prefix("https://"))?;
    authority_host(authority)?;
    Some(authority)
}

/// Browser developers use different local app ports. Trust literal loopback
/// names, never DNS resolution: a foreign hostname may resolve to loopback.
fn dev_request(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
    if !shared.personas { return Ok(()); }
    let host = single_header(request, "host");
    let origin = single_header(request, "origin");
    if !request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|addr| addr.ip().is_loopback())
        || !request.url().starts_with('/') || request.url().starts_with("//")
        || !matches!(host, Ok(Some(host)) if local_authority(host))
        || !matches!(origin, Ok(None) | Ok(Some(_)))
        || origin.ok().flatten().is_some_and(|origin| !origin_authority(origin).is_some_and(local_authority)) {
        return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "development HTTP requires a loopback Host and a loopback browser Origin; use localhost or a literal loopback address").guide("personas"));
    }
    Ok(())
}

// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.2 — public routes bypass dev admission, never the privacy boundary.
pub(crate) fn trusted_dev_request(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> bool {
    shared.personas && dev_request(request, shared).is_ok()
}

pub(crate) fn cors(request: &Request) -> Vec<Header> {
    let mut headers = vec![
        Header::from_bytes("Vary", "Origin").unwrap(),
        Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Expose-Headers", "X-Snapback-Time, X-Snapback-Clock, X-Snapback-Generation").unwrap(),
        Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "content-type, x-snapback-persona, x-snapback-meter, authorization, range, x-snapback-device").unwrap(),
        Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, POST, OPTIONS").unwrap(),
    ];
    if let Ok(Some(origin)) = single_header(request, "origin") && origin_authority(origin).is_some() {
        headers.push(Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", origin).unwrap());
    }
    if latency::headers_enabled() {

//! Read checkout is independent of the schema-adoption writer.
use super::*;

// @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — reader reuse retains fresh transactions and retires old native modules.
/// Reuse only detached read owners. Each query still opens and rolls back
/// its own identity-checked SQLite transaction; no result or viewer is cached.
#[derive(Default)]
pub(super) struct QueryReaders {
    pub(super) epoch: Arc<()>,
    pub(super) idle: Vec<Engine>,
    pub(super) source: Option<Engine>,
    memory: bool,
    pub(super) refusal_sites: Arc<dev_refusals::Sites>,
    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(super) fail_open_after: Option<usize>,
    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(super) retry_open: Option<Arc<dyn Fn() + Send + Sync>>,
    candidate_queries: BTreeSet<String>,
    launching_feed: bool,
}

impl QueryReaders {
    pub(super) fn take(
        &mut self,
        engine: &Engine,
    ) -> Result<(Arc<()>, Engine), snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
        self.memory = engine.store().conn().path().is_none_or(str::is_empty);
        if self.source.is_none() {
            self.source = engine.reader().ok();
        }
        let reader = match self.idle.pop() {
            Some(reader) => reader,
            None => engine.reader()?,
        };
        Ok((self.epoch.clone(), reader))
    }

    pub(super) fn put(&mut self, epoch: &Arc<()>, reader: Engine) {
        if Arc::ptr_eq(epoch, &self.epoch) && self.idle.len() < REQUEST_WORKERS {
            self.idle.push(reader);
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn invalidate(&mut self) {
        // Native code can change without a schema-generation change. Loans
        // retain their old epoch so an in-flight return cannot repopulate it.
        self.epoch = Arc::new(());
        self.idle.clear();
        self.source = None;
        self.candidate_queries.clear();
        self.launching_feed = false;
    }
}

impl QueryReaders {
    pub(super) fn new(engine: &Engine, sites: &dev_refusals::Sites) -> Self {
        Self {
            source: engine.reader().ok(),
            refusal_sites: Arc::new(sites.clone()),
            memory: engine.store().conn().path().is_none_or(str::is_empty),
            ..Self::default()
        }
    }
    pub(super) fn replace(&mut self, engine: &Engine, sites: &dev_refusals::Sites) {
        self.invalidate();
        self.refusal_sites = Arc::new(sites.clone());
        self.memory = engine.store().conn().path().is_none_or(str::is_empty);
        self.source = engine.reader().ok();
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — retry a failed factory on existing writer work; reads still refuse immediately.
    pub(super) fn retry_missing(&mut self, engine: &Engine) {
        if !self.memory && self.source.is_none() {
            self.source = latency::acquire(|| {
                #[cfg(test)]
                if let Some(open) = &self.retry_open { open(); }
                engine.reader()
            }).ok();
        }
    }
    pub(super) fn begin_adoption(&mut self, candidate: &Bound) {
        self.launching_feed = self.source.as_ref().is_some_and(|old| candidate.schema.timelines.iter()
            .any(|next| !old.schema().timelines.iter().any(|t| t.same_declaration(next))));
        self.candidate_queries = candidate
            .programs
            .iter()
            .filter(|p| p.program.kind == ProgramKind::Query)
            .map(|p| p.program.name.clone())
            .collect();
    }
    pub(super) fn finish_adoption(&mut self) {
        self.candidate_queries.clear();
        self.launching_feed = false;
    }
    pub(super) fn launching_feed(&self) -> bool { self.launching_feed }
    pub(super) fn pending(&self, name: &str) -> bool {
        self.candidate_queries.contains(name)
    }
    fn detached(&mut self) -> Option<Result<(Arc<()>, Engine), snapback4_core::RuntimeError>> {
        #[cfg(test)]
        if let Some(remaining) = &mut self.fail_open_after {
            if *remaining == 0 {
                self.idle.clear();
                return Some(Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Store("injected reader-open failure; retry the request".into())));
            }
            *remaining -= 1;
        }
        let source = self.source.as_ref()?;
        Some(match self.idle.pop() {
            Some(reader) => Ok((self.epoch.clone(), reader)),
            None => source.reader().map(|reader| (self.epoch.clone(), reader)),
        })
    }
}
impl Shared {
    // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — a fresh WAL snapshot reads the old program during atomic activation.
    // The source is only a connection factory: no transaction, answer or viewer
    // is retained. Identity guards still refuse an obsolete generation.
    pub(super) fn reader(&self) -> Result<(Arc<()>, TimedReader), snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
        let (result, sites) = self.acquire_reader(|| {
            let start = trace::begin();
            let mut readers = lock(&self.readers);
            trace::waited(start, "readers");
            (readers.detached(), readers.refusal_sites.clone())
        });
        if let Some(result) = result {
            return result.map(|(epoch, reader)| (epoch, TimedReader { engine: reader, refusal_sites: sites, _timing: latency::EngineSpan::new() }));
        }
        // A missing factory can also mean opening a file-backed reader failed.
        // Never acquire the writer to repair that failure under a read permit.
        Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Store("detached reader unavailable; retry the request after the store is available".into()))
    }
    fn acquire_reader<T>(&self, open: impl FnOnce() -> T) -> T {
        latency::acquire(|| {
            #[cfg(test)]
            test_work(&self.reader_open);
            open()
        })
    }
    pub(super) fn open_reader(&self, engine: &Engine) -> Result<Engine, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
        self.acquire_reader(|| engine.reader())
    }
    pub(super) fn take_reader(&self, engine: &Engine) -> Result<(Arc<()>, Engine), snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
        self.acquire_reader(|| lock(&self.readers).take(engine))
    }
    pub(super) fn memory_store(&self) -> bool { lock(&self.readers).memory }

    // Identity, head and generation are captured from one reader incarnation.
    pub(super) fn read_identity(&self) -> Result<(String, u64, u64), Refusal> {
        match self.reader() {
            Ok((epoch, mut reader)) => {
                let generation = reader.generation();
                let head = response_head(&mut reader).map(|(id, head)| (id, head, generation));
                lock(&self.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
                head
            }
            Err(_) if self.memory_store() => {
                let mut engine = lock(&self.engine);
                let generation = engine.generation();
                response_head(&mut engine).map(|(id, head)| (id, head, generation))
            }
            Err(error) => Err(error.refusal()),
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn read_head(&self) -> Result<(String, u64), Refusal> {
        match self.reader() {
            Ok((epoch, mut reader)) => {
                let head = response_head(&mut reader);
                lock(&self.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
                head
            }
            Err(_) if self.memory_store() => response_head(&mut lock(&self.engine)),
            Err(error) => Err(error.refusal()),
        }
    }
}

/// `--fresh` still dies with its host, but a private WAL file permits detached
/// readers during a schema save. Only this guard removes its own directory.
pub(super) struct FreshDirectory(std::path::PathBuf);
impl FreshDirectory {
    pub(super) fn new() -> Result<Self, String> {
        let id =
            snapback4_core::ids::mint(snapback4_core::now_ms(), &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default())
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let path =
            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("snapback4-fresh-{}-{id}", std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let owned = Self(path);
        snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&owned.0).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        Ok(owned)
    }
    pub(super) fn store(&self) -> Result<snapback4_core::Store, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
        snapback4_core::Store::open(&self.0.join("store.db"))
    }
}
impl Drop for FreshDirectory {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
    }
}

// Ownership ends on return to the pool or on an error/drop (including sync).
pub(super) struct TimedReader { engine: Engine, pub(super) refusal_sites: Arc<dev_refusals::Sites>, _timing: latency::EngineSpan }
impl TimedReader {
    pub(super) fn into_inner(self) -> Engine { self.engine }
}
impl std::ops::Deref for TimedReader {
    type Target = Engine;
    fn deref(&self) -> &Engine { &self.engine }
}
impl std::ops::DerefMut for TimedReader {
    fn deref_mut(&mut self) -> &mut Engine { &mut self.engine }
}
