/bin/bash -lc 'timeout 10s cat snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/actions.rs' in /home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b16-stage
 succeeded in 0ms:
//! The request waits here; the engine never calls the application's worker.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::engine::actions::{ActionClaim, ActionRequest, ActionSettled, SESSION_SECONDS};

fn failure(error: &snapback4_core::RuntimeError) -> Refusal {
    error.refusal()
}

fn decode_id(encoded: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let mut bytes = Vec::with_capacity(encoded.len());
    let mut chars = encoded.bytes();
    while let Some(byte) = chars.next() {
        bytes.push(if byte == b'%' {
            let high = (chars.next()? as char).to_digit(16)?;
            let low = (chars.next()? as char).to_digit(16)?;
            (high * 16 + low) as u8
        } else { byte });
    }
    String::from_utf8(bytes).ok()
}

fn outcome(run: &ActionRequest, replayed: bool) -> Json {
    let mut body = match run.state.as_str() {
        "done" => json!({ "state": "done", "id": run.id, "result": run.result }),
        "failed" | "parked" => {
            let code = run.error_code.as_deref().unwrap_or("E_ACTION_FAILED");
            let mapped = snapback4_lang::diag::guide_for(code);
            // Only the closed OIDC settlement set maps to services here.
            // E_AUTH and every non-services mapping retain the legacy action guide.
            let guide = if mapped == "services" && code != "E_AUTH" { mapped } else { "actions" };
            json!({ "state": "failed", "id": run.id, "why": Refusal::new(code, "action", run.error.as_deref().unwrap_or("the handler failed")).guide(guide) })
        }
        _ => {
            let mut why = Refusal::new("E_ACTION_PENDING", "action", "the action is still pending; retry with the same id to collect its result").guide("actions");
            why.retryable = true;
            json!({ "state": "pending", "id": run.id, "why": why })
        }
    };
    if replayed { body["replayed"] = json!(true); }
    body
}

fn claim_json(claim: &ActionClaim) -> Json {
    let run = &claim.request;
    let session = &claim.session;
    json!({ "id": run.id, "action": run.action, "args": wire::map_to_json(&run.args), "principal": run.principal, "attempt": run.attempt, "at": run.at, "contract": run.contract, "deadline": run.deadline, "now": claim.now, "session": { "principal": session.principal, "kind": session.kind, "token": session.token, "expiresAt": session.expires_at } })
}

fn arguments(program: &snapback4_core::Program, json: &Json) -> Result<BTreeMap<String, snapback4_core::Value>, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
    let fields = json.as_object().ok_or_else(|| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input("action args must be an object".into()))?;
    fields.iter().map(|(name, value)| {
        let kind = program.args.get(name).ok_or_else(|| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input(format!("undeclared action argument {name}")))?;
        wire::from_json(kind, value).map(|value| (name.clone(), value)).map_err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input)
    }).collect()
}

pub(super) fn call(request: Request, shared: &Shared, name: &str, body: &str) {
    let principal = match principal_of(&request, shared) { Ok(p) => p.0, Err(why) => return respond(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why })) };
    let payload: Json = match serde_json::from_str(body) { Ok(Json::Object(p)) => Json::Object(p), _ => return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "an action takes { id, args }") })) };
    let Some(id) = payload.get("id").and_then(Json::as_str) else { return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "mint an id on the client and reuse it when retrying this action") })); };
    let admitted = {
        let mut engine = producing_engine(shared);
        if let Err(why) = intent_store(&engine, &payload) {
            let metadata = response_head(&mut engine);
            drop(engine);
            return respond(request, 200, match metadata {
                Ok((store_id, head)) => json!({ "denied": why, "store_id": store_id, "head": head }),
                Err(why) => json!({ "denied": why }),
            });
        }
        let raw_args = payload.get("args").cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| json!({}));
        match engine.replay_action(id, &principal, name, &raw_args, shared.now()) {
            Ok(Some(run)) => Ok((run, true)),
            Err(error) => Err(error),
            Ok(None) => {
                let args = engine.program(name).filter(|p| p.kind == ProgramKind::Action).ok_or_else(|| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_ACTION_UNKNOWN", message: format!("no action {name} is declared") })
                    .and_then(|p| {
                        if let Some(why)=super::dev_doors::persona_argument(shared,&p.args,&raw_args) {Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input(why.message))}
                        else {arguments(p, &raw_args)}
                    });
                args.and_then(|args| {
                    let mut context = Context::new(&principal, args).with_wire_args(raw_args).at(shared.now());
                    if let Ok(token) = crate::auth::bearer_token(&request) { context = context.with_session(token); }
                    engine.request_action(name, id, context)
                })
            }
        }
    };
    let (mut run, replayed) = match admitted { Ok(run) => run, Err(error) => { let why = failure(&error); return respond(request, if why.retryable { 503 } else { 200 }, json!({ "denied": why })); } };
    shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine));
    let wait = query_params(request.url()).get("wait").and_then(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(25).min(25);
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(wait);
    while !matches!(run.state.as_str(), "done" | "failed" | "parked") && Instant::now() < deadline {
        {
            let seq = lock(&shared.seq);
            let _ = shared.committed.wait_timeout(seq, deadline.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now()).min(Duration::from_millis(250))).unwrap_or_else(|p| p.into_inner());
        }
        // The caller's session may expire or be revoked while the handler runs.
        if let Err(why) = principal_of(&request, shared) { return respond(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why })); }
        run = match producing_engine(shared).action_request_at(id, &principal, shared.now()) { Ok(run) => run, Err(error) => return respond(request, if error.refusal().retryable { 503 } else { 200 }, json!({ "denied": failure(&error) })) };
    }
    if let Err(why) = principal_of(&request, shared) { return respond(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why })); }
    respond(request, if matches!(run.state.as_str(), "done" | "failed" | "parked") { 200 } else { 202 }, outcome(&run, replayed));
}

pub(super) fn claim(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    claim_selected(request, shared, body, false)
}

pub(super) fn claim_set(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    claim_selected(request, shared, body, true)
}

fn claim_selected(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str, registry: bool) {
    let payload: Json = match serde_json::from_str(body) { Ok(Json::Object(p)) => Json::Object(p), _ => return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "claim takes an object") })) };
    let names: Option<Vec<String>> = if registry {
        // The existing whole-request byte bound applies. Do not shorten the
        // accepted operation-name surface: private route names have a prefix.
        let names = payload.get("actions").and_then(Json::as_array).and_then(|values| values.iter().map(|value| value.as_str().filter(|name| !name.is_empty()).map(str::to_owned)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>());
        match names {
            Some(names) if !names.is_empty() && names.len() <= 128 && payload.get("action").is_none() => Some(names),
            _ => return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "claim-set takes actions: 1–128 nonempty names, without action") })),
        }
    } else { None };
    let action = payload.get("action").and_then(Json::as_str);
    let limit = payload.get("limit").and_then(Json::as_u64).unwrap_or(1).clamp(1, 100) as usize;
    let lease = payload.get("lease").and_then(Json::as_i64).unwrap_or(60).clamp(1, 900) * 1_000;
    let session = payload.get("session").and_then(Json::as_i64).unwrap_or(SESSION_SECONDS);
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(payload.get("wait").and_then(Json::as_u64).unwrap_or(0).min(25));
    loop {
        let (worker, claimed) = {
            let mut engine = producing_engine(shared);
            let worker = match service_of_locked(&request, shared, &engine) { Ok(worker) => worker, Err(why) => return respond(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why })) };
            let claimed = match &names {
                Some(names) => engine.claim_action_set(&worker, names, limit, lease, session, shared.now()),
                None => engine.claim_actions(&worker, action, limit, lease, session, shared.now()),
            };
            (worker, claimed)
        };
        if claimed.as_ref().is_ok_and(|runs| !runs.is_empty()) { shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine)); }
        match claimed {
            Ok(claims) if claims.is_empty() && Instant::now() < deadline => {
                let seq = lock(&shared.seq);
                let _ = shared.committed.wait_timeout(seq, deadline.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now()).min(Duration::from_secs(1))).unwrap_or_else(|p| p.into_inner());
            }
            Ok(claims) => return respond(request, 200, json!({ "requests": claims.iter().map(claim_json).collect::<Vec<_>>(), "worker": worker })),
            Err(error) => return respond(request, if error.refusal().retryable { 503 } else { 200 }, json!({ "denied": failure(&error) })),
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn settle(request: Request, shared: &Shared, path: &str, body: &str) {
    if let Some(id) = path.strip_prefix("/actions/").and_then(|path| path.strip_suffix("/cancel")).and_then(decode_id) {
        let principal = match principal_of(&request, shared) { Ok(p) => p.0, Err(why) => return respond(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why })) };
        let answer = producing_engine(shared).cancel_action(&id, &principal, shared.now());
        return match answer {
            Ok(run) => { shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine)); respond(request, 200, json!({ "ok": true, "request": outcome(&run, false) })); }
            Err(error) => respond(request, if error.refusal().retryable { 503 } else { 200 }, json!({ "denied": failure(&error) })),
        };
    }
    let worker = match service_of(&request, shared) { Ok(worker) => worker, Err(why) => return respond(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why })) };
    let token = match single_header(&request, "x-snapback-action-lease") { Ok(Some(token)) => token.to_owned(), _ => return respond(request, 200, json!({ "denied": Refusal::new("E_ACTION_LEASE", "action", "settle with the claim's session.token in x-snapback-action-lease").guide("actions") })) };
    let payload: Json = match serde_json::from_str(body) { Ok(Json::Object(p)) => Json::Object(p), _ => return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "settlement takes { result } or { error, retry }") })) };
    if let Some(tail) = path.strip_prefix("/actions/") {
        if let Some(id) = tail.strip_suffix("/status").and_then(decode_id) {
            let answer = producing_engine(shared).action_status(&id, &worker, &token, shared.now());
            return match answer {
                Ok(run) => respond(request, 200, json!({ "invocation": { "id": run.id, "attempt": run.attempt, "deadline": run.deadline, "cancelled": run.cancelled, "state": run.state, "errorCode": run.error_code } })),
                Err(error) => respond(request, if error.refusal().retryable { 503 } else { 200 }, json!({ "denied": failure(&error) })),
            };
        }
        if let Some((id, tail)) = tail.split_once("/steps/") {
            if let Some((name, verb)) = tail.rsplit_once('/') {
                if let (Some(id), Some(name)) = (decode_id(id), decode_id(name)) {
                    let field = match verb { "begin" => Some("input"), "done" => Some("result"), _ => None };
                    if field.is_some_and(|field| payload.get(field).is_none()) {
                        return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "a step begin requires input and a step done requires result (null is explicit)") }));
                    }
                    let answer = {
                        let mut engine = producing_engine(shared);
                        match verb {
                            "begin" => engine.begin_action_step(&id, &worker, &token, &name, payload.get("input").unwrap_or(&Json::Null), payload.get("reconcile").and_then(Json::as_bool).unwrap_or(false), shared.now()).map(|step| json!({ "step": step })),
                            "done" => engine.complete_action_step(&id, &worker, &token, &name, payload.get("result").unwrap_or(&Json::Null), shared.now()).map(|step| json!({ "ok": true, "step": step })),
                            "park" => engine.park_action_step(&id, &worker, &token, &name, payload.get("error").and_then(Json::as_str).unwrap_or("the provider outcome is unknown"), shared.now()).map(|()| json!({ "ok": true })),
                            _ => Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input("step routes are begin, done or park".into())),
                        }
                    };
                    shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine));
                    return match answer {
                        Ok(answer) => respond(request, 200, answer),
                        Err(error) => respond(request, if error.refusal().retryable { 503 } else { 200 }, json!({ "denied": failure(&error) })),
                    };
                }
            }
        }
    }
    let (id, done) = if let Some(id) = path.strip_prefix("/actions/").and_then(|p| p.strip_suffix("/done")) { (id, true) }
        else if let Some(id) = path.strip_prefix("/actions/").and_then(|p| p.strip_suffix("/failed")) { (id, false) }
        else { return respond(request, 404, json!({ "denied": Refusal::new("E_ACTION_UNKNOWN", "action", "no action settlement at this path") })); };
    let settled = if done { ActionSettled::Done(payload.get("result").cloned().unwrap_or(Json::Null)) }
        else {
            let error = payload.get("error").and_then(Json::as_str).unwrap_or("the handler failed").to_owned();
            let retry = payload.get("retry").and_then(Json::as_bool).unwrap_or(true);
            let code = match payload.get("code").and_then(Json::as_str) {
                Some("E_SECRET_MISSING") => Some("E_SECRET_MISSING"), Some("E_ACTION_GENERATION") => Some("E_ACTION_GENERATION"),
                Some("E_ACTION_RESULT") => Some("E_ACTION_RESULT"), Some("E_STEP_INPUT") => Some("E_STEP_INPUT"),
                Some("E_ROUTE_RESULT") => Some("E_ROUTE_RESULT"),
                Some("E_STEP_RESULT") => Some("E_STEP_RESULT"), Some("E_STEP_PARKED") => Some("E_STEP_PARKED"),
                Some("E_ACTION_CANCELLED") => Some("E_ACTION_CANCELLED"), Some("E_ACTION_DEADLINE") => Some("E_ACTION_DEADLINE"),
                Some("E_ACTION_CLOSED") => Some("E_ACTION_CLOSED"), Some("E_WRITE_LABEL") => Some("E_WRITE_LABEL"),
                Some("E_OIDC_BINDING") => Some("E_OIDC_BINDING"), Some("E_OIDC_CALLBACK") => Some("E_OIDC_CALLBACK"),
                Some("E_OIDC_CONFIG") => Some("E_OIDC_CONFIG"), Some("E_OIDC_EXCHANGE") => Some("E_OIDC_EXCHANGE"),
                Some("E_OIDC_HANDOFF") => Some("E_OIDC_HANDOFF"), Some("E_OIDC_MINT") => Some("E_OIDC_MINT"),
                Some("E_OIDC_PROVIDER") => Some("E_OIDC_PROVIDER"), Some("E_OIDC_RECIPE") => Some("E_OIDC_RECIPE"),
                Some("E_OIDC_REPLAY") => Some("E_OIDC_REPLAY"), Some("E_OIDC_TRANSACTION") => Some("E_OIDC_TRANSACTION"),
                _ => None,
            };
            if !retry && code.is_some() { ActionSettled::Refused { code: code.unwrap(), error } } else { ActionSettled::Failed { error, retry } }
        };
    let Some(id) = decode_id(id) else { return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "the action id must be percent-encoded UTF-8") })); };
    let answer = producing_engine(shared).settle_action(&id, &worker, &token, settled, shared.now());
    match answer {
        Ok(run) => {
            if !done && shared.personas { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: action {} {} attempt {} settled {}: {}", run.action, run.id, run.attempt, run.state, serde_json::to_string(&run.error).unwrap_or_default()); }
            shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine)); respond(request, 200, json!({ "ok": true, "request": outcome(&run, false) }));
        }
        Err(error) => { let why = failure(&error); respond(request, if why.retryable { 503 } else { 200 }, json!({ "denied": why })); }
    }
}

/// Whether `POST /assets` carries `as=file`.
pub fn is_file_upload(url: &str) -> bool {
    crate::serve::query_params(url).get("as").is_some_and(|kind| kind == "file")
}

/// The most bytes a file upload may stream: the schema's largest file
/// bound, under the general upload ceiling. A schema with no file column
/// refuses before any body is read.
pub fn file_ceiling(shared: &Shared) -> Result<usize, Refusal> {
    match producing_engine(shared).file_upload_ceiling() {
        Some(max) => Ok((max as usize).min(UPLOAD_CEILING)),
        None => Err(Refusal::new("E_ASSET_TYPE", "asset", "no column of this backend takes a file; declare one (`doc: file <=10000000`)").guide("assets")),
    }
}

pub fn upload(request: Request, body: Vec<u8>, shared: &Shared) {
    let principal = match principal_of(&request, shared) {
        Ok(principal) => principal.0,
        Err(refusal) => return respond(request, 401, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) })),
    };
    if body.is_empty() {
        return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_ASSET_TYPE", "asset", "the upload is empty: POST the file's bytes as the body").guide("assets")) }));
    }
    let params = crate::serve::query_params(request.url());
    let file = match params.get("as").map(String::as_str) {
        None | Some("image" | "video") => None,
        Some("file") => Some(params.get("name").cloned().unwrap_or_default()),
        Some(other) => return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("as={other} is not an upload kind; use as=file for a document, or omit it for an image or video")).guide("assets")) })),
    };
    let declared = match crate::serve::single_header(&request, "content-type") {
        Ok(value) => snapback4_core::assets::declared_type(value),
        Err(()) => return respond(request, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "send one Content-Type header").guide("assets")) })),
    };
    let intent = params.get("intent").map(String::as_str);
    if intent.is_some_and(|id| id.is_empty() || id.len() > 128 || !id.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'-' || b == b'_')) {
        return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied": Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "intent is 1–128 ASCII letters, digits, hyphens or underscores")}));
    }
    use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
    let fingerprint = snapback4_core::value::digest(&(format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(&body)), &file.as_ref().map(|name| snapback4_core::assets::sanitize_name(name)), &declared));
    let expected_store = params.get("store_id");
    if intent.is_some() && expected_store.is_none() { return respond(request,400,json!({"denied":Refusal::new("E_INPUT","input","intent uploads require store_id from the identified backend")})); }
    let stored = (|| {
        if let Some(expected) = expected_store {
            if producing_engine(shared).store().meta("store_id")?.as_ref() != Some(expected) {
                return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_STORE_REPLACED", message: "upload intent belongs to another backend store".into() });
            }
        }
        if let Some(id) = intent {
            if let Some(asset) = producing_engine(shared).asset_intent(&principal, id, &fingerprint)? { return Ok(asset); }
        }
        let placement=if let Some(id)=intent {Some(producing_engine(shared).asset_declaration(&principal,id)?.ok_or_else(||snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Asset{code:"E_UPLOAD_INTENT",message:"declare this intent's immutable placement before sending its bytes".into()} )?)}else{None};
        let upload = match &file {
            // @ref LLP 3000.038#2 — the declared type is verified against the bytes before admission.
            Some(name) => {
                let content_type = snapback4_core::assets::verify_file(&body, declared.as_deref())?;
                producing_engine(shared).prepare_file(&principal, content_type, name, body.len() as u64, shared.now())?
            }
            None => {
                let sniffed = snapback4_core::assets::sniff(&body)?;
                producing_engine(shared).prepare_asset(&principal, sniffed, body.len() as u64, shared.now())?
            }
        };
        #[cfg(test)] crate::serve::test_work(&shared.upload_work);
        let upload = if let Some(id) = intent { upload.with_intent(id, &fingerprint).with_placement(placement.unwrap()) } else { upload };
        let upload = upload.write(&body)?;
        // Logout while disk I/O was in flight cannot publish a new asset.
        principal_of(&request, shared).map_err(|_| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Auth("sign in again".into()))?;
        let mut engine = producing_engine(shared);
        crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());
        if let Some(expected) = expected_store {
            if engine.store().meta("store_id")?.as_ref() != Some(expected) { return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Structural {code:"E_STORE_REPLACED",message:"upload store changed during disk write".into()}); }
        }
        engine.finish_asset(upload)
    })();
    match stored {
        Ok(asset) => {
            let mut facts = json!({ "id": asset.id, "type": asset.content_type, "bytes": asset.bytes, "width": asset.width, "height": asset.height, "duration": asset.duration });
            if let Some(name) = asset.name { facts["name"] = json!(name); }
            respond(request, 200, json!({ "asset": facts }))
        }
        Err(error) => {
            let refusal = error.refusal();
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!("{} at POST /assets: {}", refusal.code, refusal.message);
            respond(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 200 }, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }))
        }
    }
}

    #[test]
    fn byte_ranges_distinguish_invalid_and_unsatisfiable_requests() {
        assert_eq!(byte_range("bytes=1-3", 10), ByteRange::Satisfiable(1, 3));
        assert_eq!(byte_range("bytes=8-30", 10), ByteRange::Satisfiable(8, 9));
        assert_eq!(byte_range("bytes=-3", 10), ByteRange::Satisfiable(7, 9));
        assert_eq!(byte_range("bytes=10-20", 10), ByteRange::Unsatisfiable);
        assert_eq!(byte_range("bytes=7-3", 10), ByteRange::Unsatisfiable);
        assert_eq!(byte_range("bytes=-0", 10), ByteRange::Unsatisfiable);
        assert_eq!(byte_range("bytes=wat", 10), ByteRange::Ignore);
        assert_eq!(byte_range("items=1-3", 10), ByteRange::Ignore);
    }
}

/// A durable cancellation tombstone closes cancellation versus staged-file commit.
pub fn cancel(request: Request, id: &str, shared: &Shared) {
    let principal = match principal_of(&request, shared) { Ok(p) => p.0, Err(why) => return respond(request, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})) };
    if id.is_empty() || id.len() > 128 || !id.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'-' || b == b'_') {
        return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":Refusal::new("E_INPUT","input","invalid upload intent id")}));
    }
    let params = crate::serve::query_params(request.url());
    let mut engine = producing_engine(shared);
    let result = if params.get("store_id") != engine.store().meta("store_id").ok().flatten().as_ref() {
        Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Structural {code:"E_STORE_REPLACED",message:"cancellation belongs to another backend store".into()})
    } else { engine.cancel_asset_intent(&principal,id) };
    drop(engine);
    match result { Ok(()) => respond(request,200,json!({"ok":true})), Err(e) => respond(request,200,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&e.refusal())})) }
}

/// Finalize only a reported terminal refusal; an existing bound execution always wins.
pub fn finish(request: Request, id: &str, body: Vec<u8>, shared: &Shared) {
    let principal = match principal_of(&request,shared) { Ok(p)=>p.0,Err(why)=>return respond(request,401,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})) };
    let params = crate::serve::query_params(request.url());
    let result = (|| {
        let payload: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&body).map_err(|e| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input(e.to_string()))?;
        if serde_json::to_vec(&payload).map_err(|e|snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input(e.to_string()))?.len()>96<<10 {return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Asset{code:"E_UPLOAD_ARGUMENT",message:"finalization envelope exceeds 96 KiB".into()});}
        let name = payload.get("op").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str).ok_or_else(|| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input("finalization needs op".into()))?;
        let args = payload.get("args").ok_or_else(|| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input("finalization needs args".into()))?;
        let why: Refusal = serde_json::from_value(payload.get("why").cloned().unwrap_or_default()).map_err(|e| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input(e.to_string()))?;
        let mut engine = producing_engine(shared);
        if params.get("store_id") != engine.store().meta("store_id")?.as_ref() { return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Structural {code:"E_STORE_REPLACED",message:"finalization belongs to another backend store".into()}); }
        engine.finish_asset_intent(&principal,id,name,args,&why)
    })();
    match result { Ok(outcome) => respond(request,200,outcome.receipt(Some(id))), Err(error)=>respond(request,200,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())})) }
}

/// Owner-only pinned-intent inventory gives storage-loss victims a bounded cleanup path.
pub fn intents(request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
    let principal = match principal_of(&request,shared) { Ok(p)=>p.0,Err(why)=>return respond(request,401,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})) };
    let params=crate::serve::query_params(request.url());
    let mut engine=producing_engine(shared);
    let result=(|| {
        if params.get("store_id") != engine.store().meta("store_id")?.as_ref() { return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Structural {code:"E_STORE_REPLACED",message:"intent inventory requires the current backend store_id".into()}); }
        let after=params.get("after").map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("");
        if after.len()>128 { return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input("invalid intent continuation".into())); }
        engine.pending_asset_intents(&principal,after)
    })();
    drop(engine);
    match result {Ok(json)=>respond(request,200,json),Err(error)=>respond(request,200,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())}))}
}

/// A bounded declaration binds byte fingerprint and placement before any byte request.
pub fn declare(request:Request,id:&str,body:Vec<u8>,shared:&Shared){
    let principal=match principal_of(&request,shared){Ok(p)=>p.0,Err(why)=>return respond(request,401,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}))};
    let params=crate::serve::query_params(request.url());
    let result=(||{
        if id.is_empty()||id.len()>128||!id.bytes().all(|b|b.is_ascii_alphanumeric()||b==b'-'||b==b'_'){return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input("invalid upload intent id".into()));}
        let payload:serde_json::Value=serde_json::from_slice(&body).map_err(|e|snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input(e.to_string()))?;
        if serde_json::to_vec(&payload).map_err(|e|snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input(e.to_string()))?.len()>96<<10 {return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Asset{code:"E_UPLOAD_ARGUMENT",message:"declaration envelope exceeds 96 KiB".into()});}
        let digest=payload["digest"].as_str().filter(|s|s.len()==64&&s.bytes().all(|b|b.is_ascii_digit()||(b'a'..=b'f').contains(&b))).ok_or_else(||snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input("declaration needs SHA-256 of the selected bytes".into()))?;
        let placement=&payload["placement"];
        let argument=placement["argument"].as_str().filter(|a|!a.is_empty()).ok_or_else(||snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input("declaration needs its asset argument".into()))?;
        if placement["op"].as_str().is_none()||!placement["args"].is_object()||placement["args"].get(argument).is_some(){return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Input("declare op and placing args omitting the asset argument".into()));}
        let file=payload.get("file").map(|f|snapback4_core::assets::sanitize_name(f["name"].as_str().unwrap_or("")));
        let declared=payload.get("file").and_then(|f|snapback4_core::assets::declared_type(f["type"].as_str()));
        let fingerprint=snapback4_core::value::digest(&(digest,file,declared));
        let mut engine=producing_engine(shared);
        if params.get("store_id")!=engine.store().meta("store_id")?.as_ref(){return Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Structural{code:"E_STORE_REPLACED",message:"declaration belongs to another backend store".into()});}
        engine.declare_asset_intent(&principal,id,&fingerprint,placement)
    })();
    match result{Ok(())=>respond(request,200,json!({"ok":true})),Err(error)=>respond(request,200,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())}))}
}

// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#1 — direct children, file logs and bounded cleanup own proof orchestration.
import { spawn, type ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
import { closeSync, mkdirSync, openSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join, resolve } from "node:path";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { setTimeout as sleep } from "node:timers/promises";
import { stripVTControlCharacters } from "node:util";

const children = new Set<ChildProcess>();
process.once("exit", () => { for (const child of children) child.kill("SIGKILL"); });
export function register(child: ChildProcess): void {
  children.add(child);
  child.once("exit", () => children.delete(child));
  child.on("error", () => children.delete(child));
}
export interface ProofOptions {
  cwd?: string;
  env?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv;
  args?: readonly string[];
  timeoutMs?: number;
  stopTimeoutMs?: number;
  logsDir?: string;
  /** Keep stdout in memory only (stderr remains diagnostic evidence). */
  secret?: boolean;
  /** Internal: a frontend keeper owns this private process group. */
  detached?: boolean;
  /** Internal: the frontend observes parent death through stdin EOF. */
  livenessPipe?: boolean;
}
export interface ProofHost {
  url: string;
  pid: number;
  logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string };
  stop(): Promise<void>;
}
export function deadline(ms: number | undefined, fallback: number): number {
  const value = ms ?? fallback;
  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 1) throw new RangeError("proof timeout must be positive and finite");
  return value;
}
export async function bounded<T>(work: Promise<T>, ms: number, label: string): Promise<T> {
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  try {
    return await Promise.race([work, new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
      timer = setTimeout(() => reject(Error(`${label} timed out after ${ms} ms`)), ms);
    })]);
  } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
}
/** Attempts every cleanup, even if a preceding step fails or stalls. */
export async function cleanup(...steps: Array<(() => unknown | Promise<unknown>) | undefined>): Promise<void> {
  const errors: unknown[] = [];
  for (const step of steps) if (step) {
    try { await bounded(Promise.resolve().then(step), 10_000, "proof cleanup"); }
    catch (error) { errors.push(error); }
  }
  if (errors.length) throw new AggregateError(errors, "proof cleanup failed");
}
export async function stopChild(child: ChildProcess | Pick<ProofHost, "url" | "stop">, graceMs = 5000): Promise<void> {
  deadline(graceMs, 5000);
  if (child && "url" in child && typeof child.url === "string" && typeof child.stop === "function") {
    await bounded(Promise.resolve().then(() => child.stop()), graceMs + 5000, "host stop"); return;
  }
  if (!child || !("kill" in child) || typeof child.kill !== "function" || typeof child.once !== "function"
      || !(child.exitCode === null || typeof child.exitCode === "number")
      || !(child.signalCode === null || typeof child.signalCode === "string")) {
    throw new TypeError("stopChild needs a ChildProcess or { url, stop }");
  }
  if (child.exitCode !== null || child.signalCode !== null) return;
  let done!: () => void;
  const exited = new Promise<void>(resolve => { done = resolve; });
  child.once("exit", done);
  child.once("error", done);
  try {
    child.kill("SIGTERM");
    try { await bounded(exited, graceMs, "SIGTERM"); }
    catch {
      child.kill("SIGKILL");
      await bounded(exited, 5000, "SIGKILL reap");
    }
  } finally { child.removeListener("exit", done); child.removeListener("error", done); }
}
export function portRange(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): string {
  const low = env.PORT_LO, high = env.PORT_HI;
  if (low === undefined && high === undefined) return "0";
  if (!low || !high || !/^\d+$/.test(low) || !/^\d+$/.test(high)
      || Number(low) < 1 || Number(high) > 65535 || Number(low) > Number(high)) {
    throw Error("PORT_LO and PORT_HI must both specify an inclusive range, 1 <= low <= high <= 65535");
  }
  return `${Number(low)}-${Number(high)}`;
}
/** App-local evidence survives harness cleanup of its temporary root. */
export function logsDir(options: Pick<ProofOptions, "cwd" | "logsDir"> = {}): string {
  return resolve(options.cwd ?? ".", options.logsDir ?? "logs");
}
export function logged(command: string, args: readonly string[], options: ProofOptions = {}) {
  const cwd = resolve(options.cwd ?? "."), id = randomUUID();
  const directory = logsDir(options); mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
  const logs = { stdout: join(directory, `${id}.stdout.log`), stderr: join(directory, `${id}.stderr.log`) };
  if (options.secret) writeFileSync(logs.stdout, "stdout withheld: secret command (returned only to caller)\n", { flag: "wx", mode: 0o600 });
  const out = options.secret ? undefined : openSync(logs.stdout, "wx", 0o600), err = openSync(logs.stderr, "wx", 0o600);
  let child: ChildProcess;
  try { child = spawn(command, [...args], { cwd, detached: options.detached ?? false, env: { ...(options.env ?? process.env), SNAPBACK4_PROOF_LAUNCH: id }, stdio: [options.livenessPipe ? "pipe" : "ignore", options.secret ? "pipe" : out!, err] }); }
  finally { if (out !== undefined) closeSync(out); closeSync(err); }
  // Secret stdout never reaches a file, even briefly. Callers still receive it.
  let stdout = "";
  child.stdout?.setEncoding("utf8");
  child.stdout?.on("data", chunk => { stdout += chunk; });
  register(child);
  child.stdin?.on("error", () => {});
  let error: Error | undefined;
  child.on("error", value => { error = value; });
  const closed = new Promise<void>(resolve => child.once("close", () => resolve()));
  return { child, logs, id, closed, stdout: () => options.secret ? stdout : logText(logs.stdout), error: () => error };
}
export const logText = (path: string): string => readFileSync(path, "utf8");
export async function ready(run: ReturnType<typeof logged>, parse: (line: string) => string | undefined, timeoutMs: number): Promise<string> {
  const end = performance.now() + timeoutMs;
  let outAt = 0, errAt = 0;
  while (performance.now() < end) {
    if (run.error()) throw run.error();
    if (run.child.exitCode !== null || run.child.signalCode !== null) {
      throw Error(`proof child exited before ready (${run.child.exitCode ?? run.child.signalCode}); ${run.logs.stderr}\n${logText(run.logs.stderr).slice(-4000)}`);
    }
    for (const [path, offset, stream] of [[run.logs.stdout, outAt, "out"], [run.logs.stderr, errAt, "err"]] as const) {
      const text = logText(path);
      const complete = text.lastIndexOf("\n") + 1;
      for (const line of text.slice(offset, complete).split("\n")) {
        const url = parse(stripVTControlCharacters(line).trim());
        if (url) return url;
      }
      if (stream === "out") outAt = complete; else errAt = complete;
    }
    await sleep(25);
  }
  throw Error(`proof child ready timed out after ${timeoutMs} ms; logs: ${run.logs.stdout}, ${run.logs.stderr}`);
}

/// Delegated credentials remain dependent on the admitted authority in flight,
/// not merely at claim time. Revocation cannot leave a live independent seat.
pub(crate) fn held_claim_authority(tx: &Tx<'_>, id: &str, worker: &str, token: &str, now: i64) -> Result<ActionRequest, RuntimeError> {
    let run = request(tx.conn(), id)?.ok_or_else(unknown)?;
    let digest = identity::digest(token);
    if !authority::held_claim_authority(tx, id, worker, &digest, now)? { return Err(lease_error()); }
    Ok(run)
}

pub(super) fn insert_request(tx: &Tx<'_>, schema: &Schema, program: &Program, id: &str, context: &Context, raw: &Json, service: bool, now: i64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    let spec = schema.action_specs.get(&program.name).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
    insert_spec(tx, program, id, context, raw, service, &spec, &spec.contract(program), now)
}

/// Routes use the same durable record and authority binding, with their
/// declaration's contract rather than a public action program's contract.
pub(super) fn insert_spec(tx: &Tx<'_>, program: &Program, id: &str, context: &Context, raw: &Json, service: bool, spec: &ActionSpec, contract: &str, now: i64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if !spec.work.policy.valid() { return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("the action policy is outside its bounds".into())); }
    let authority = context.session_digest.as_deref().map(|digest| identity::authority(tx, digest, &context.viewer, now)).transpose()?;
    let service_generation = if service { Some(identity::principal_generation(tx, &context.viewer)?) } else { None };
    let release: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'release'", [], |row| row.get(0)).optional()?;
    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _requests(id, name, args, principal, at, state, family, principal_generation, admitted_expires_at, args_digest, spec, contract, deadline, release, service_generation) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, 'queued', ?6, ?7, ?8, ?9, ?10, ?11, ?12, ?13, ?14)",
        params![id, program.name, serde_json::to_string(&context.args).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Input(e.to_string()))?, context.viewer, now,
            authority.as_ref().map(|a| &a.family), authority.as_ref().map(|a| a.generation), authority.as_ref().map(|a| a.expires_at), crate::store::payload_digest(raw),
            serde_json::to_string(&spec).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Schema(e.to_string()))?, contract, spec.work.policy.deadline_ms.map(|ms| now.saturating_add(ms as i64)), release, service_generation])?;
    Ok(())
}

impl Engine {
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#9.2 — durable action admission precedes the worker and survives retries by id.
    pub fn request_action(&mut self, name: &str, id: &str, mut context: Context) -> Result<(ActionRequest, bool), RuntimeError> {
        if context.viewer == crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL || !crate::value::identity_fits(&context.viewer) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Auth("an action needs a signed-in principal".into()));
        }
        if id.len() > 128 || !crate::value::identity_fits(id) || id.starts_with(super::routes::ID_PREFIX) { return Err(RuntimeError::Input("an action id is 1–128 printable characters, minted by the client; route:v1: is reserved for admitted routes".into())); }
        let now = context.now.unwrap_or_else(crate::now_ms);
        let wire_args = context.wire_args.take().unwrap_or_else(|| Json::Object(crate::wire::map_to_json(&context.args)));
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        if let Some(recorded) = replay(&tx, id, &context.viewer, name, &wire_args, now)? {
            tx.rollback()?;
            return Ok((recorded, true));
        }
        let program = self.programs.get(name).ok_or_else(unknown)?;
        validate::validate_inputs(&self.schema, program, &mut context.args)?;
        authorize(program, &context.viewer)?;
        let args = serde_json::to_string(&context.args).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Input(e.to_string()))?;
        if args.len() as u64 > self.ceilings.heap_bytes { return Err(RuntimeError::Input("action arguments exceed the heap bound".into())); }
        insert_request(&tx, &self.schema, program, id, &context, &wire_args, false, now)?;
        let run = request(tx.conn(), id)?.ok_or_else(unknown)?;
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok((run, false))
    }

    pub fn action_request(&self, id: &str, principal: &str) -> Result<ActionRequest, RuntimeError> {
        self.action_request_at(id, principal, crate::now_ms())
    }

    pub fn action_request_at(&self, id: &str, principal: &str, now: i64) -> Result<ActionRequest, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.store.authority_transaction()?;
        let run = request(tx.conn(), id)?.filter(|run| run.principal == principal).ok_or_else(unknown)?;
        if authority_expiry(&tx, &run, now)?.is_none() { return Err(unknown()); }
        tx.rollback()?;
        Ok(run)
    }

    pub fn replay_action(&self, id: &str, principal: &str, name: &str, args: &Json, now: i64) -> Result<Option<ActionRequest>, RuntimeError> {
        // The public action fast path must not collect private route responses
        // before ingress has authenticated this delivery (including a replay).
        if id.starts_with(super::routes::ID_PREFIX) || name.starts_with(crate::schema::routes::ACTION_PREFIX) { return Err(unknown()); }
        let tx = self.store.authority_transaction()?;
        let answer = replay(&tx, id, principal, name, args, now)?;
        tx.rollback()?;
        Ok(answer)
    }

    /// `session_seconds` is chosen per action by the worker. A lease ends
    /// at the earlier of its requested duration and the viewer session's.

    fn claim_selected_actions(&mut self, worker: &str, action: Option<&str>, names: Option<&str>, limit: usize, lease_ms: i64, session_seconds: i64, now: i64) -> Result<Vec<ActionClaim>, RuntimeError> {
        if !identity::is_service(worker) { return Err(RuntimeError::Auth("actions are claimed by a service".into())); }
        let duration = lease_ms.clamp(1_000, MAX_LEASE_MS).min(session_seconds.clamp(1, MAX_LEASE_MS / 1_000) * 1_000);
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        expire(&tx, now)?;
        let ids: Vec<String> = tx.conn().prepare("SELECT id FROM _requests WHERE state = 'queued' AND (next_at IS NULL OR next_at <= ?1) AND (?2 IS NULL OR name = ?2) AND (?4 IS NULL OR name IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?4))) ORDER BY at, id LIMIT ?3")?.query_map(params![now, action, limit.clamp(1, 100) as i64, names], |row| row.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
        let mut claims = Vec::new();
        for id in ids {
            let run = request(tx.conn(), &id)?.ok_or_else(unknown)?;
            if let Some(contract) = &run.contract {
                let current = if let Some(name) = run.action.strip_prefix(crate::schema::routes::ACTION_PREFIX) {
                    self.schema.routes.get(name).map(|route| route.contract(name))
                } else { self.programs.get(&run.action).map(|program| self.schema.action_specs.get(&run.action).cloned().unwrap_or_default().contract(program)) };
                if current.as_ref() != Some(contract) {
                    finish(&tx, &id, "parked", "E_ACTION_GENERATION", "the admitted action declaration changed; inspect this work before replacing it", now)?;
                    continue;
                }
            }
            // @ref LLP 3000.009#5.1 — a claim borrows only authority that still stands.
            let Some(expiry) = authority_expiry(&tx, &run, now)? else {
                tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _requests SET state = 'failed', error_code = 'E_ACTION_AUTHORITY', error = ?2, settled_at = ?3, next_at = NULL, lease_until = NULL, claimed_by = NULL, session_digest = NULL WHERE id = ?1", params![id, AUTHORITY_ERROR, now])?;
                continue;
            };
            let session = identity::action_session(tx.conn(), &run.principal, now, now.saturating_add(duration).min(expiry).min(run.deadline.unwrap_or(i64::MAX)))?;
            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _requests SET state = 'running', attempts = attempts + 1, claimed_by = ?2, session_digest = ?3, lease_until = ?4, next_at = NULL, error = NULL, error_code = NULL WHERE id = ?1", params![id, worker, identity::digest(&session.token), session.expires_at])?;
            claims.push(ActionClaim { request: request(tx.conn(), &id)?.ok_or_else(unknown)?, session, now });
        }
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(claims)
    }

codex
The tests cover slow-body and queued requests for seed, reset, clock, and job doors, plus replacement-reader auth checks. I found a possible gap in action admission: it authenticates a persona before acquiring the engine writer, then queues work without rechecking that authority. I’m tracing the core behavior to confirm the impact.

//! A declared route authenticates each delivery before joining durable work.
//! Unverified bytes have a bounded, ephemeral ticket, never a database seat.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::{engine::{actions::ActionRequest, routes::verification_token}, schema::{RouteSpec, routes::{RouteRequest, RouteResponse, BODY_BYTES}}, value::encode_bytes_hex};

#[path = "serve_route_verify.rs"]
mod verification;
pub(super) use verification::{claim, settle};

#[derive(Default)]
pub(super) struct Verification { tickets: Mutex<BTreeMap<String, Ticket>> }
struct Ticket {
    name: String,
    contract: String,
    generation: i64,
    envelope: RouteRequest,
    deadline: Instant,
    deadline_ms: i64,
    lease: Option<Lease>,
    outcome: Option<Verified>,
}
struct Lease { worker: String, token: String, until: Instant }
#[derive(Clone)]
enum Verified { Allowed { id: String, principal: String }, Denied, Unavailable }
struct TicketGuard<'a> { registry: &'a Verification, id: String }
impl Drop for TicketGuard<'_> {
    fn drop(&mut self) { lock(&self.registry.tickets).remove(&self.id); }
}

pub(super) fn declared(shared: &Shared, url: &str) -> Option<(String, RouteSpec)> {
    lock(&shared.bound).schema.routes.iter().find(|(_, route)| route.path == url).map(|(name, route)| (name.clone(), route.clone()))
}

fn refuse(request: Request, status: u16, code: &'static str, message: &str) {
    let mut why = Refusal::new(code, "route", message).guide("routes");
    why.retryable = status >= 500;
    respond_with_cors(request, status, json!({ "denied": why }), false);
}
fn unavailable(request: Request) {
    refuse(request, 503, "E_ROUTE_PENDING", "the route has no settled response; retry the same body");
}
fn pause(shared: &Shared, deadline: Instant) {
    let seq = lock(&shared.seq);
    let _ = shared.committed.wait_timeout(seq, deadline.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now()).min(Duration::from_millis(100))).unwrap_or_else(|p| p.into_inner());
}

/// Bearer sessions are valid on declared public routes even in dev. Personas
/// still pass through the full loopback Host/Origin/peer checks.
fn principal(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<String, Refusal> {
    if !matches!(single_header(request, "x-snapback-persona"), Ok(None)) { return principal_of(request, shared).map(|c| c.0); }
    let token = crate::auth::bearer_token(request).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?;
    let engine = lock(&shared.engine);
    snapback4_core::identity::resolve_supervised(engine.store(), &token, snapback4_core::identity::ServiceSample { now: shared.now(), supervisor_revision: shared.worker_revision.load(Ordering::Acquire) }).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?
        .map(|(principal, _, _)| principal).ok_or_else(|| snapback4_core::identity::diagnostics::refused(engine.store(), &token, shared.now(), trusted_dev_request(request, shared)))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.009#5.1 — every provider delivery verifies before durable admission or result collection.
pub(super) fn ingress(mut request: Request, shared: &Shared, name: &str, route: RouteSpec) {
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_millis(route.wait_ms);
    let deadline_ms = shared.now().saturating_add(route.wait_ms as i64);
    let contract = route.contract(name);
    if route.service.is_none() && let Err(why) = principal(&request, shared) {
        return respond_with_cors(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why }), false);
    }
    // A persona is never a provider credential, even on loopback.
    if route.service.is_some() && !matches!(single_header(&request, "x-snapback-persona"), Ok(None)) {
        return refuse(request, 401, "E_ROUTE_VERIFY", "a provider route requires its signature, not a development persona");
    }
    let mut envelope = RouteRequest { method: "POST".into(), path: route.path.clone(),
        headers: request.headers().iter().map(|h| (h.field.as_str().to_string(), h.value.as_str().to_owned())).collect(), body_hex: String::new() };
    if envelope.validate(&route).is_err() { return refuse(request, 431, "E_ROUTE_INPUT", "route headers exceed their bound or are invalid"); }
    let expected = request.body_length();
    if expected.is_some_and(|n| n > BODY_BYTES) { return refuse(request, 413, "E_ROUTE_INPUT", "a route body exceeds 64 KiB"); }
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    let mut buffer = [0; 8192];
    loop {
        let width = buffer.len().min(BODY_BYTES - bytes.len() + 1);
        match request.as_reader().read(&mut buffer[..width]) {
            Ok(0) => break,
            Ok(n) if n > BODY_BYTES - bytes.len() => return refuse(request, 413, "E_ROUTE_INPUT", "a route body exceeds 64 KiB"),
            Ok(n) => bytes.extend_from_slice(&buffer[..n]),
            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::Interrupted => continue,
            Err(_) => return refuse(request, 400, "E_ROUTE_INPUT", "the route body could not be read"),
        }
    }
    if expected.is_some_and(|n| n != bytes.len()) { return refuse(request, 400, "E_ROUTE_INPUT", "the route body ended before Content-Length"); }
    envelope.body_hex = encode_bytes_hex(&bytes);
    drop(bytes);
    if Instant::now() >= deadline || shared.now() >= deadline_ms { return unavailable(request); }
    let (mut run, principal) = if route.service.is_some() {
        let authority = {let engine=lock(&shared.engine);crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());engine.route_service_generation(name, &contract)};
        let generation = match authority {
            Ok((_, generation)) => generation, Err(_) => return unavailable(request),
        };
        let id = match verification_token() { Ok(id) => id, Err(_) => return unavailable(request) };
        {
            let mut tickets = lock(&shared.route_verification.tickets);
            if tickets.len() >= ROUTE_WORKERS { drop(tickets); return unavailable(request); }
            tickets.insert(id.clone(), Ticket { name: name.into(), contract: contract.clone(), generation, envelope,
                deadline, deadline_ms, lease: None, outcome: None });
        }
        let guard = TicketGuard { registry: &shared.route_verification, id };
        shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine));
        let (id, principal) = loop {
            let outcome = lock(&shared.route_verification.tickets).get(&guard.id).and_then(|ticket| ticket.outcome.clone());
            match outcome {
                Some(Verified::Allowed { id, principal }) => break (id, principal),
                Some(Verified::Denied) => return refuse(request, 403, "E_ROUTE_VERIFY", "the provider signature was refused"),
                Some(Verified::Unavailable) => return unavailable(request),
                None if Instant::now() >= deadline || shared.now() >= deadline_ms || crate::lifecycle::stopping() => return unavailable(request),
                None => pause(shared, deadline),
            }
        };
        drop(guard);
        let collected = {let mut engine=lock(&shared.engine);crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());engine.route_request_at(name, &contract, &id, &principal, shared.now())};
        match collected { Ok(run) => (run, principal), Err(_) => return unavailable(request) }
    } else {
        let principal = match principal(&request, shared) { Ok(p) => p, Err(why) => return respond_with_cors(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why }), false) };
        let mut context = Context::new(&principal, BTreeMap::new()).at(shared.now());
        if let Ok(token) = crate::auth::bearer_token(&request) { context = context.with_session(token); }
        let admitted = {let mut engine=lock(&shared.engine);crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());engine.request_session_route(name, &contract, &envelope, context)};
        match admitted { Ok((run, _)) => (run, principal), Err(error) => return respond_with_cors(request, if error.refusal().family == "auth" { 401 } else { 503 }, json!({ "denied": error.refusal() }), false) }
    };
    shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine));
    loop {
        if route.service.is_none() && let Err(why) = self::principal(&request, shared) {
            return respond_with_cors(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why }), false);
        }
        // Recheck even an immediately settled replay: revocation and adoption
        // must not turn a retained response into a new authority grant.
        let collected = {let mut engine=lock(&shared.engine);crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());engine.route_request_at(name, &contract, &run.id, &principal, shared.now())};
        run = match collected {
            Ok(run) => run, Err(_) => return unavailable(request),
        };
        if run.state == "done" { return completed(request, &run); }
        if matches!(run.state.as_str(), "failed" | "parked") || Instant::now() >= deadline || shared.now() >= deadline_ms || crate::lifecycle::stopping() { return unavailable(request); }
        pause(shared, deadline);
    }
}

fn completed(request: Request, run: &ActionRequest) {
    let response = run.result.clone().and_then(|value| serde_json::from_value::<RouteResponse>(value).ok());
    let Some(response) = response else { return unavailable(request); };
    let Ok(bytes) = response.validate() else { return unavailable(request); };
    if response.status >= 500 { return unavailable(request); }

fn target(dir: &Path) -> Result<Option<Owner>, String> {
    match deployment_lock(dir) {
        Ok(owner) => Ok(Some(owner)),
        Err(error) if error.contains("E_STORE_BUSY") => Ok(None),
        Err(error) => Err(error),
    }
}
fn status(
    dir: &Path,
    owner: Option<&Owner>,
    after: u64,
    items_after: Option<String>,
    event: Option<u64>,
) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
    match owner {
        Some(owner) => owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .and_then(|mut s| s.release_status(after, items_after.as_deref(), event))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        None => crate::release_control::request(
            dir,
            crate::release_control::Command::Status {
                after,
                items_after,
                event,
            },
        ),
    }
}
fn activate(
    dir: &Path,
    owner: Option<&Owner>,
    release: u64,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    revision: u64,
    bound: snapback4_lang::Bound,
    rollback: bool,
    restore_dormant: bool,
    allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    options: &Options,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let root = dir.join("releases").join(release.to_string());
    let digest = crate::release_files::digest(&root)?;
    if let Some(owner) = owner {
        let project = Project { root };
        let prepared = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project, &bound.schema)?;
        crate::release_files::link_store(&project.root)?;
        crate::release_files::durable(&project.root)?;
        let mut pointer = crate::release_files::Pointer::prepare(dir, release, previous, rollback)?;
        let activation = snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation {
            release,
            expected_previous: previous,
            expected_revision: Some(revision),
            restore_dormant,

//! Request/response I/O outside the data plane. Durable admission is at most
//! once; a handler's delivery is at least once, under a fenced viewer lease.
use super::{Context, Engine, effects::MAX_LEASE_MS, validate};
use crate::{RuntimeError, authority::{self, AuthorityTransaction}, identity, ir::{BinaryOp, Expr, Input, Program, ProgramKind, Stmt, UnaryOp}, store::Tx, value::Value};
use crate::schema::{ActionSpec, Schema};
use rusqlite::{Connection, OptionalExtension, params};
use serde_json::Value as Json;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

pub const SESSION_SECONDS: i64 = 60;

mod steps;
pub use steps::ActionStep;

pub(crate) fn initialize(conn: &Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    conn.execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _requests (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT NOT NULL, args TEXT NOT NULL, principal TEXT NOT NULL,
        at INTEGER NOT NULL, state TEXT NOT NULL, attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        next_at INTEGER, lease_until INTEGER, claimed_by TEXT, session_digest TEXT,
        result TEXT, error TEXT);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS _requests_due ON _requests(state, name, at, id);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS _requests_session ON _requests(session_digest);")?;
    let columns: Vec<String> = conn.prepare("SELECT name FROM pragma_table_info('_requests')")?.query_map([], |row| row.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
    for (column, kind) in [("family", "TEXT"), ("principal_generation", "INTEGER"), ("admitted_expires_at", "INTEGER"), ("args_digest", "TEXT"), ("error_code", "TEXT"), ("settled_at", "INTEGER"), ("spec", "TEXT"), ("contract", "TEXT"), ("deadline", "INTEGER"), ("release", "TEXT"), ("service_generation", "INTEGER"), ("cancelled", "INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")] {
        if !columns.iter().any(|name| name == column) { conn.execute_batch(&format!("ALTER TABLE _requests ADD COLUMN {column} {kind};"))?; }
    }
    steps::initialize(conn)?;
    Ok(())
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct ActionRequest {
    pub id: String,
    pub action: String,
    pub args: BTreeMap<String, Value>,
    pub principal: String,
    pub at: i64,
    pub state: String,
    pub attempt: u32,
    pub lease_until: Option<i64>,
    pub result: Option<Json>,
    pub error: Option<String>,
    pub error_code: Option<String>,
    pub authority: Option<identity::Authority>,
    pub args_digest: Option<String>,
    pub settled_at: Option<i64>,
    pub spec: ActionSpec,
    pub contract: Option<String>,
    pub deadline: Option<i64>,
    pub release: Option<String>,
    pub service_generation: Option<i64>,
    pub cancelled: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ActionClaim {
    pub request: ActionRequest,
    pub session: identity::Session,
    pub now: i64,
}

pub enum ActionSettled {
    Done(Json),
    Failed { error: String, retry: bool },
    Refused { code: &'static str, error: String },
}

const COLUMNS: &str = "id, name, args, principal, at, state, attempts, lease_until, result, error, error_code, family, principal_generation, admitted_expires_at, args_digest, settled_at, spec, contract, deadline, release, service_generation, cancelled";
fn read(row: &rusqlite::Row<'_>) -> rusqlite::Result<ActionRequest> {
    fn decode<T: serde::de::DeserializeOwned>(text: String, col: usize) -> rusqlite::Result<T> {
        serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|error| rusqlite::Error::FromSqlConversionFailure(col, rusqlite::types::Type::Text, Box::new(error)))
    }
    Ok(ActionRequest { id: row.get(0)?, action: row.get(1)?, args: decode(row.get(2)?, 2)?, principal: row.get(3)?, at: row.get(4)?, state: row.get(5)?, attempt: row.get::<_, u32>(6)?, lease_until: row.get(7)?, result: row.get::<_, Option<String>>(8)?.map(|s| decode(s, 8)).transpose()?, error: row.get(9)?, error_code: row.get(10)?,
        authority: match (row.get::<_, Option<String>>(11)?, row.get::<_, Option<i64>>(12)?, row.get::<_, Option<i64>>(13)?) {
            (Some(family), Some(generation), Some(expires_at)) => Some(identity::Authority { family, generation, expires_at }),
            _ => None,
        }, args_digest: row.get(14)?, settled_at: row.get(15)?,
        spec: row.get::<_, Option<String>>(16)?.map(|s| decode(s, 16)).transpose()?.unwrap_or_default(),
        contract: row.get(17)?, deadline: row.get(18)?, release: row.get(19)?, service_generation: row.get(20)?, cancelled: row.get(21)?, })
}
pub(super) fn request(conn: &Connection, id: &str) -> Result<Option<ActionRequest>, RuntimeError> {
    Ok(conn.query_row(&format!("SELECT {COLUMNS} FROM _requests WHERE id = ?1"), [id], read).optional()?)
}
fn unknown() -> RuntimeError { RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_ACTION_UNKNOWN", message: "no action or request with this name and id is available to this viewer".into() } }
fn lease_error() -> RuntimeError { RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_ACTION_LEASE", message: "this action claim is not live; claim again and settle with its session token in x-snapback-action-lease".into() } }

pub const AUTHORITY_ERROR: &str = "the authority that admitted this action was revoked or has expired";

pub(super) fn authority_expiry(tx: &Tx<'_>, run: &ActionRequest, now: i64) -> Result<Option<i64>, RuntimeError> {
    let Some(work) = tx.work_by_id(&run.id)? else { return Ok(None) };
    authority::work_authority_expiry(tx, &work, now)
}

fn replay(tx: &Tx<'_>, id: &str, principal: &str, name: &str, args: &Json, now: i64) -> Result<Option<ActionRequest>, RuntimeError> {
    let Some(run) = request(tx.conn(), id)? else { return Ok(None); };
    if run.principal != principal || authority_expiry(tx, &run, now)?.is_none() { return Err(unknown()); }
    if run.action != name { return Err(if run.args_digest.is_some() { crate::store::id_reused() } else { unknown() }); }
    if let Some(digest) = &run.args_digest {
        if crate::store::payload_digest(args) != *digest { return Err(crate::store::id_reused()); }
    }
    Ok(Some(run))
}

/// An action's compiled body is only its rule. Refuse any other IR rather
/// than accidentally running data-plane statements as an action.
/// Shared by declaration checking and runtime admission; schedules must not
/// pass `check` only to fail the identical static rule during activation.
pub fn authorize(program: &Program, viewer: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    fn value(expr: &Expr, viewer: &str) -> Option<Value> {
        Some(match expr {
            Expr::Literal(v @ (Value::Bool(_) | Value::Id(_))) => v.clone(),
            Expr::Input(Input::Viewer) => Value::Id(viewer.into()),
            Expr::Unary { op: UnaryOp::Not, value: v } => match value(v, viewer)? { Value::Bool(v) => Value::Bool(!v), _ => return None },
            Expr::Binary { op, left, right } => {
                let (a, b) = (value(left, viewer)?, value(right, viewer)?);
                Value::Bool(match (op, a, b) {
                    (BinaryOp::Eq, a, b) => a == b,
                    (BinaryOp::Ne, a, b) => a != b,
                    (BinaryOp::And, Value::Bool(a), Value::Bool(b)) => a && b,
                    (BinaryOp::Or, Value::Bool(a), Value::Bool(b)) => a || b,
                    _ => return None,
                })
            }
            _ => return None,
        })
    }
    if program.kind != ProgramKind::Action { return Err(unknown()); }
    let allowed = match program.body.as_slice() { [Stmt::Require { condition, .. }] => value(condition, viewer) == Some(Value::Bool(true)), _ => false };
    if !allowed { return Err(RuntimeError::Require { code: "E_RULE".into(), site: Some(format!("actions.{}", program.name)), message: None }); }
    Ok(())

use super::*;

#[test]
fn atomic_feed_launch_keeps_queries_reading_and_holds_sync_and_writes() {
    let directory = ReaderTestDirectory::new();
    let next = concat!("use personas alice, bob\n", include_str!("../../../snapback4-lang/tests/fixtures/following-view.q"));
    let before = next.replace("view feed: activity where .actor = viewer or exists follows[viewer, .actor]\n", "")
        .replace("return feed last", "return activity last")
        .replace("{ *, post: posts[.post] }", "{ * }");
    let host = Host::with_store(true, snapback4_core::Store::open(&directory.0.join("feed.sqlite")).unwrap(), &before);
    let headers = "x-snapback-persona: alice\r\n";
    assert_eq!(body(&host.request("POST", "/m/post", headers, r#"{"id":"01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FA0","args":{"postId":"p","parentId":null}}"#))["state"], "sent");
    let old = *lock(&host.shared.generation);
    let (entered, waiting) = mpsc::channel(); let (resume, resumed) = mpsc::channel();
    {
        let engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
        engine.store().conn().commit_hook(Some(move || {
            entered.send(()).unwrap(); resumed.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(10)).unwrap(); false
        })).unwrap();
    }
    let shared = host.shared.clone();
    let task = thread::spawn(move || {
        let b = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),next.into())]);
        let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
        let (generation, _) = adoption::adopt_candidate(&shared, &mut engine, &b, || panic!("unexpected reopen")).unwrap();
        engine.store().conn().commit_hook(None::<fn()->bool>).unwrap();
        lock(&shared.readers).replace(&engine, &b.refusal_sites);
        *lock(&shared.bound) = b; *lock(&shared.generation) = generation;
    });
    waiting.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(10)).unwrap();
    let request = |path: &'static str, payload: String| {
        let address=host.address; let (sent,received)=mpsc::channel();
        let handle=thread::spawn(move || {
            let response=exchange_held(address, Duration::from_secs(10), &format!("POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nx-snapback-persona: alice\r\n\r\n{payload}",payload.len()));
            sent.send(response).unwrap();
        }); (received,handle)
    };
    let syncs: Vec<_> = (0..32).map(|_| request("/sync",format!(r#"{{"generation":{old},"from":0,"limit":100}}"#))).collect();
    wait_until(|| host.shared.engine.waiting() == 32);
    // All 32 syncs have reached the held writer, not merely the listener.
    // Query and discovery must still read the predecessor with filled slots uncommitted.
    let query = host.request("POST", "/q/home", headers, r#"{"args":{"c":null}}"#);
    assert!(!query.is_empty(), "32 waiting syncs exhausted query admission");
    assert_eq!(status(&query),200,"{query}"); assert!(!query.contains("$timeline:"));
    assert_eq!(body(&query)["data"].as_array().unwrap().len(),1);
    let schema = host.request("GET", "/schema", headers, "");
    assert_eq!(status(&schema), 200, "{schema}");
    assert_eq!(body(&schema)["generation"], old);
    assert!(!schema.contains("$timeline:"));
    assert_eq!(host.admission.syncs.load(Ordering::Acquire), 32);
    let refused = host.request("POST", "/sync", headers, "{}");
    assert_eq!(status(&refused), 503, "sync admission remains bounded");
    assert_eq!(body(&refused)["denied"]["code"], "E_RATE_LIMIT");
    let (write, write_thread)=request("/m/post",r#"{"id":"01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FA1","args":{"postId":"after","parentId":null}}"#.into());
    assert!(syncs.iter().all(|(sync, _)| sync.try_recv().is_err()));
    assert!(write.recv_timeout(Duration::from_millis(100)).is_err());
    resume.send(()).unwrap(); task.join().unwrap();
    for (sync, sync_thread) in syncs {
        let sync=sync.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(3)).unwrap();
        assert!(!sync.contains("$timeline:")); assert_eq!(body(&sync)["denied"]["code"],"E_SYNC_GENERATION");
        sync_thread.join().unwrap();
    }
    let write=write.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(3)).unwrap(); assert_eq!(body(&write)["state"],"sent","{write}");
    write_thread.join().unwrap();
    let retry=host.request("POST","/m/post",headers,r#"{"id":"01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FA1","args":{"postId":"after","parentId":null}}"#);
    assert_eq!(body(&retry)["state"],"sent");
    assert_eq!(lock(&host.shared.engine).store().count("posts").unwrap(),2,"same-id retry crosses adoption once");
}

#[test]
fn atomic_feed_sync_authenticates_before_wait_and_rechecks_after_it() {
    for revoke in [false, true] {
        let directory = ReaderTestDirectory::new();
        let next = format!("use identity\n{}", include_str!("../../../snapback4-lang/tests/fixtures/following-view.q"));
        let before = next.replace("view feed: activity where .actor = viewer or exists follows[viewer, .actor]\n", "")
            .replace("return feed last", "return activity last");
        let host = Host::with_store(false, snapback4_core::Store::open(&directory.0.join("auth.sqlite")).unwrap(), &before);
        let engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
        let session = snapback4_core::identity::service_session(engine.store(), "reader", host.shared.now(), 1).unwrap();
        let candidate = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), next)]);
        assert!(candidate.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", candidate.diagnostics);
        lock(&host.shared.readers).begin_adoption(&candidate);
        for headers in ["", "Authorization: Bearer invalid\r\n"] {
            let reply = host.request("POST", "/sync", headers, "{}");
            assert_eq!(status(&reply), 401, "authentication must finish before the held writer: {reply}");
            assert_eq!(body(&reply)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH");
            assert_eq!(host.shared.engine.waiting(), 0);
        }
        let address = host.address;
        let sync = thread::spawn(move || exchange_held(address, WAIT_TIMEOUT, &format!("POST /sync HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nAuthorization: Bearer {}\r\nContent-Length: 2\r\n\r\n{{}}", session.token)));
        wait_until(|| host.shared.engine.waiting() == 1);
        engine.store().conn().execute(if revoke { "DELETE FROM _sessions" } else { "UPDATE _sessions SET expires_at = 0" }, []).unwrap();
        lock(&host.shared.readers).finish_adoption();
        drop(engine);
        let reply = sync.join().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(status(&reply), 401, "authority changed while waiting: {reply}");
        assert_eq!(body(&reply)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH");
    }
}

#[test]
fn a2_adoption_fences_supervised_claims_before_the_old_child_stops() {
    let schema = "use personas alice\nuse server\naction work() returns { n: int } <- allow\neffect mail\nmutation emitMail():\n  emit mail {n: 1}\n";
    let host = Host::with_store(true, snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap(), schema);
    let (old, manual) = {
        let engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
        (crate::dev_worker::child_session(engine.store(), host.shared.now(), 0).unwrap(),
         snapback4_core::identity::service_session(engine.store(), "manual", host.shared.now(), 1).unwrap())
    };
    let old_headers = format!("Authorization: Bearer {}\r\n", old.token);
    assert_eq!(status(&host.request("POST", "/actions/claim", &old_headers, "{}")), 200);
    // Keep the old credential alive (as if its child ignores SIGTERM) while
    // activating and publishing the new revision under the claim's lock.
    {
        let mut engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), schema.replace("n: int }", "n: int, ok: bool ? }"))]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let generation = engine.adopt(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone())).unwrap();
        *lock(&host.shared.bound) = bound;
        *lock(&host.shared.generation) = generation;
        host.shared.worker_revision.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Release);
        assert!(snapback4_core::identity::resolve(engine.store(), &old.token, host.shared.now()).unwrap().is_some());
    }
    let call = body(&host.request("POST", "/a/work?wait=0", "x-snapback-persona: alice\r\n", r#"{"id":"adoption-work","args":{}}"#));
    assert_eq!(call["state"], "pending", "{call}");
    let mutation = body(&host.request("POST", "/m/emitMail", "x-snapback-persona: alice\r\n", r#"{"id":"01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FAV","args":{}}"#));
    assert_eq!(mutation["state"], "sent", "{mutation}");
    for path in ["/actions/claim", "/effects/claim"] {
        let refused = host.request("POST", path, &old_headers, "{}");
        assert_eq!(status(&refused), 401, "{path}: {refused}");
        assert_eq!(body(&refused)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH");
    }
    // Unsupervised seats are not accidentally fenced with the child, and
    // the new child may claim the untouched new admission exactly once.
    let manual_headers = format!("Authorization: Bearer {}\r\n", manual.token);
    assert_eq!(body(&host.request("POST", "/effects/claim", &manual_headers, "{}"))["effects"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 1);
    let current = {
        let engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
        crate::dev_worker::clear_child_session(engine.store()).unwrap();
        crate::dev_worker::child_session(engine.store(), host.shared.now(), 1).unwrap()
    };
    let current_headers = format!("Authorization: Bearer {}\r\n", current.token);
    let claimed = body(&host.request("POST", "/actions/claim", &current_headers, "{}"));
    assert_eq!(claimed["requests"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 1, "{claimed}");
    assert_eq!(claimed["requests"][0]["attempt"], 1);
}

#[test]
fn w8r2_adoption_bounds_alternating_background_reasons() {
    let mut state = adoption::Status::new("store".into());
    let first = snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Store("expiry phase is locked".into());
    let second = snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Store("job phase is locked".into());
    state.refused(&first, 1);
    let mut lines = Vec::new();
    for _ in 0..80 {
        for (name, error) in [("the expiry sweep", &first), ("job", &second)] {
            if let Some(line) = state.background_failure(name, error) { lines.push(line); }
        }
    }
    assert_eq!(lines.len(), 2, "alternating reasons must share the per-code limit");
    assert!(lines[0].contains("expiry phase is locked"));
    assert!(lines[1].contains("job phase is locked"));
    assert_eq!(state.recovered().as_deref(), Some("snapback4 dev: adoption recovered; suppressed 158 repeated background failures"));
    assert!(state.recovered().is_none());
    println!("alternating adoption logs: ticks=80 failures=160 printed=2 suppressed=158");
}

#[test]
fn w8r2_adoption_rate_windows_are_per_code_and_do_not_slide_on_suppression() {
    let mut state = adoption::Status::new("store".into());
    let errors = [
        [snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Store("expiry phase".into()), snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Store("job phase".into())],
        [snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Generation("expiry owner".into()), snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Generation("job owner".into())],
    ];
    state.refused(&errors[0][0], 1);
    let start = Instant::now();
    let mut printed = 0;
    for tick in 0..120 {
        // The last suppressed tick is 1 ms before the next window opens.
        let elapsed = if tick % 60 == 59 { Duration::from_millis(tick * 1000 + 999) } else { Duration::from_secs(tick) };
        for pair in &errors {
            for error in pair {
                let line = state.background_failure_at("job", error, start + elapsed);
                assert_eq!(line.is_some(), tick % 60 == 0, "tick {tick}, {error}");
                printed += usize::from(line.is_some());
            }
        }
    }
    // An expired window alone does not make an identical reason worth printing.
    for pair in &errors {
        assert!(state.background_failure_at("job", &pair[1], start + Duration::from_secs(180)).is_none());
    }
    assert_eq!(printed, 8);
    assert_eq!(state.recovered().as_deref(), Some("snapback4 dev: adoption recovered; suppressed 474 repeated background failures"));
    println!("adoption rate windows: seconds=60 burst=2 codes=2 windows=2 failures=482 printed=8 suppressed=474");
}
