    49	/// The durable facts the authority kernel may read in one store transaction.
    50	/// Implementations perform access only; the policy below interprets records.
    51	pub(crate) trait AuthorityTransaction {
    52	    fn principal_generation(&self, principal: &str) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError>;
    53	    fn session_by_digest(&self, digest: &str) -> Result<Option<SessionRecord>, RuntimeError>;
    54	    fn latest_live_family_member(
    55	        &self,
    56	        principal: &str,
    57	        family: &str,
    58	        now: i64,
    59	    ) -> Result<Option<SessionRecord>, RuntimeError>;
    60	    fn work_by_id(&self, id: &str) -> Result<Option<WorkRecord>, RuntimeError>;
    61	    fn work_by_claimed_session_digest(
    62	        &self,
    63	        digest: &str,
    64	    ) -> Result<Option<WorkRecord>, RuntimeError>;
    65	
    66	    fn invalidate_session(&self, digest: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>;
    67	    fn invalidate_family(&self, principal: &str, family: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>;
    68	}
    69	
    70	pub(crate) enum AdmissionStanding {
    71	    SessionNotLive,
    72	    AuthorityNotLive,
    73	    Live(Authority),
    74	}
    75	
    76	/// Resolve the credential captured at admission. Refresh may replace its row,
    77	/// but the family and principal generation remain the durable authority.
    78	// @ref LLP 3000.009#5.1 — action authority is bound at admission and walked inside one transaction.
    79	pub(crate) fn admission_authority(
    80	    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
    81	    token_digest: &str,
    82	    principal: &str,
    83	    now: i64,
    84	) -> Result<AdmissionStanding, RuntimeError> {
    85	    let Some(session) = tx.session_by_digest(token_digest)? else {
    86	        return Ok(AdmissionStanding::SessionNotLive);
    87	    };
    88	    if session.scope != SessionScope::Full || session.principal != principal || session.expires_at <= now {
    89	        return Ok(AdmissionStanding::SessionNotLive);
    90	    }
    91	    let generation = tx.principal_generation(principal)?;
    92	    let Some(standing) = family_authority_expiry(tx, principal, &session.family, generation, now)?
    93	    else {
    94	        return Ok(AdmissionStanding::AuthorityNotLive);
    95	    };
    96	    Ok(AdmissionStanding::Live(Authority {
    97	        family: session.family,
    98	        generation,
    99	        expires_at: session.expires_at.min(standing),
   100	    }))
   101	}
   102	
   103	/// Walk a refresh/delegation family at one transaction snapshot. The admitted
   104	/// expiry is deliberately not an ongoing cap: refresh lifetime and admission
   105	/// lifetime are distinct. Claim leases and deadlines remain hard caps.
   106	pub(crate) fn family_authority_expiry(
   107	    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
   108	    principal: &str,
   109	    family: &str,
   110	    generation: i64,
   111	    now: i64,
   112	) -> Result<Option<i64>, RuntimeError> {
   113	    let current_generation = tx.principal_generation(principal)?;
   114	    let (mut family, mut generation, mut bound) = (family.to_owned(), generation, i64::MAX);
   115	    let mut seen = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
   116	    loop {
   117	        if generation != current_generation || !seen.insert(family.clone()) {
   118	            return Ok(None);
   119	        }
   120	        let Some(session) = tx.latest_live_family_member(principal, &family, now)? else {
   121	            return Ok(None);
   122	        };
   123	        if session.scope != SessionScope::Full { return Ok(None); }
   124	        bound = bound.min(session.expires_at);
   125	        if session.kind != "action" {
   126	            return Ok(Some(bound));
   127	        }
   128	        let Some(parent) = tx.work_by_claimed_session_digest(&session.digest)? else {
   129	            return Ok(None);
   130	        };
   131	        if parent.principal != principal || !claim_window_is_live(&parent, now) {
   132	            return Ok(None);
   133	        }
   134	        let lease = parent.lease_until.expect("a live claim has a lease");
   135	        bound = bound.min(lease).min(parent.deadline.unwrap_or(i64::MAX));
   136	        if let Some(service_generation) = parent.service_generation {
   137	            return Ok((service_generation == current_generation).then_some(bound));
   138	        }
   139	        match (parent.admission.family, parent.admission.generation) {
   140	            (Some(next), Some(next_generation)) => {
   141	                family = next;
   142	                generation = next_generation;
   143	            }
   144	            (None, None) => return Ok(Some(bound)), // Legacy work and development personas.
   145	            _ => return Ok(None),
   146	        }
   147	    }
   148	}
   149	
   150	pub(crate) fn work_authority_expiry(
   151	    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
   152	    work: &WorkRecord,
   153	    now: i64,
   154	) -> Result<Option<i64>, RuntimeError> {
   155	    if let Some(generation) = work.service_generation {
   156	        return Ok((tx.principal_generation(&work.principal)? == generation).then_some(i64::MAX));
   157	    }
   158	    match (&work.admission.family, work.admission.generation, work.admission.expires_at) {
   159	        (Some(family), Some(generation), Some(_)) => family_authority_expiry(
   160	            tx,
   161	            &work.principal,
   162	            family,
   163	            generation,
   164	            now,
   165	        ),
   166	        _ => Ok(Some(i64::MAX)), // Legacy rows and development personas.
   167	    }
   168	}
   169	
   170	fn claim_window_is_live(work: &WorkRecord, now: i64) -> bool {
   171	    work.state == "running"
   172	        && work.lease_until.is_some_and(|lease| lease > now)
   173	        && work.deadline.is_none_or(|deadline| deadline > now)
   174	}
   175	
   176	pub(crate) fn claim_is_owned(work: &WorkRecord, worker: &str, digest: &str) -> bool {
   177	    work.claimed_by.as_deref() == Some(worker) && work.session_digest.as_deref() == Some(digest)
   178	}
   179	
   180	pub(crate) fn live_claim_authority(
   181	    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
   182	    digest: &str,
   183	    now: i64,
   184	) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   185	    let Some(work) = tx.work_by_claimed_session_digest(digest)? else {
   186	        return Ok(false);
   187	    };
   188	    Ok(claim_window_is_live(&work, now) && work_authority_expiry(tx, &work, now)?.is_some())
   189	}
   190	
   191	pub(crate) fn held_claim_authority(
   192	    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
   193	    id: &str,
   194	    worker: &str,
   195	    digest: &str,
   196	    now: i64,
   197	) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   198	    let Some(work) = tx.work_by_id(id)? else {
   199	        return Ok(false);
   200	    };
   201	    if !claim_is_owned(&work, worker, digest) {
   202	        return Ok(false);
   203	    }
   204	    live_claim_authority(tx, digest, now)
   205	}
   206	
   207	pub(crate) fn live_session(
   208	    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
   209	    digest: &str,
   210	    now: i64,
   211	) -> Result<Option<SessionRecord>, RuntimeError> {
   212	    let Some(session) = tx.session_by_digest(digest)? else {
   213	        return Ok(None);
   214	    };
   215	    if session.expires_at <= now {
   216	        return Ok(None);
   217	    }
   218	    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.3 — revocation generations survive an older executable.
   219	    if session.scope == SessionScope::Read
   220	        && (session.cap_expires_at.is_none_or(|cap| cap <= now)
   221	            || session.principal_generation.is_none()
   222	            || session.principal_generation.unwrap() < tx.principal_generation(&session.principal)?) {
   223	        return Ok(None);
   224	    }
   225	    if session.kind == "action" && !live_claim_authority(tx, digest, now)? {
   226	        return Ok(None);
   227	    }
   228	    Ok(Some(session))
   229	}
   230	
   231	pub(crate) fn live_service_session(
   232	    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
   233	    digest: &str,
   234	    principal: &str,
   235	    now: i64,
   236	) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   237	    Ok(tx.session_by_digest(digest)?.is_some_and(|session| {
   238	        session.scope == SessionScope::Full && session.principal == principal && session.kind == "service" && session.expires_at > now
   239	    }))
   240	}
   241	
   242	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, serde::Serialize)]
   243	#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
   244	pub enum SessionScope { Full, Read }

 succeeded in 0ms:

codex
**NOT READY** — reviewed candidate `a87106a9e76a6f7256000a0d6ac91fdc4954fe15` against `sb4-loop`.

**[P2] Queued persona requests can still admit durable actions after password-only adoption closes persona authority.**

In [actions.rs:58](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b16-stage/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/actions.rs:58), authentication precedes acquiring the engine writer at line 62. After acquiring it, the handler performs replay/admission without calling `mutation_authority`. Persona requests supply no session digest, so core admission creates unbound work; [authority.rs:166](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b16-stage/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/authority.rs:166) continues treating that work as authorized.

A source-level reproducer is:

1. Start dev with ordinary doors and a declared persona.
2. Hold the engine writer and submit a persona-authenticated `POST /a/<action>`.
3. Once it queues for the writer, adopt password-only identity without `--unsafe-dev-doors`.
4. Release the writer. The request durably queues the action under the replacement schema—even an action introduced by that adoption.

The later authentication check can return 401, but the queued work remains claimable and can execute external effects with a delegated viewer session. This exceeds the documented allowance for reads retaining their existing snapshots.

Session routes have the same gap at [serve_routes.rs:118](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b16-stage/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/serve_routes.rs:118): persona authentication occurs before the writer acquisition and `request_session_route` call at line 121.

**Repair:** Revalidate the original principal with `mutation_authority` while holding the actual engine writer, before action replay/admission and session-route admission. Apply the same boundary to persona-authorized cancellation. Add deterministic writer-barrier regressions for password-only adoption and cast removal, asserting that refused requests create no durable work and yield nothing claimable. The current staging regression tests cover fixture/clock/job doors and replacement query readers, but miss these paths.

The inspected store classification, production bulk-seed refusal, protected local token protocol, and first-admin recipe otherwise support the intended separation. The roster independently restricts grants to `service:operator`; signup grants no role.

This is an implementation finding, independent of outstanding qualification. I inspected source and tests without executing Cargo, the full suite, or file-producing tests. Packed-consumer, Darwin, and broader qualification results remain unverified by this review. No files were edited.
